### [88] [Can statistical models capture Mamdani's success? Social choice, ranked-choice voting, and model fit, with an application to the 2025 New York City Democratic Primary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23800)
*Michael Pearce,Erin R. Lipman,Christopher Adolph,Elena A. Erosheva*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文从统计视角研究排序投票（ranked-choice voting）的可理性化模型（rationalizable models），证明该类模型对投票数据施加了不现实的分布假设、拟合效果差，无法用于统计推断；相反，非可理性化模型能更有效地揭示选民异质性偏好。作者将方法应用于2025年纽约市民主党市长初选数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会选择理论中的可理性化文献将投票规则与最大似然估计联系起来，但缺乏从统计角度系统评估这些模型对真实投票数据的拟合能力和推断价值。本文旨在填补这一空白，检验可理性化模型是否真正适用于政治选举中的偏好分布分析。

Method: 作者首先从统计视角扩展可理性化模型的现有理论结果，将其推广到更贴合实际的投票情境，并推导出模型估计与评估所需的统计细节；然后将这些模型应用于2025年纽约市民主党市长初选的排序投票数据，比较可理性化与非可理性化模型在拟合优度和解释选民异质性偏好方面的表现。

Result: 研究发现：（1）可理性化模型对投票数据施加了不现实的分布假设，拟合效果差；（2）非可理性化模型能更有意义地揭示异质性选民偏好；（3）可理性化模型无法捕捉政治选举中偏好分布的关键特征，剥夺了分析师依赖统计建模进行推断的基本用途。

Conclusion: 可理性化模型不足以作为投票数据的统计建模工具；相反，通过仔细关注合理的（plausible）数据生成机制，统计模型能够捕捉细微的选民偏好。这一结论为社会选择理论与统计建模之间的连接提供了新的视角，并为实践分析者提供了方法论指引。

Abstract: Ranked-choice voting is increasingly prevalent in elections. An extensive literature in social choice theory -- the study of collective decision-making with the goal of making compromises among disparate opinions -- considers theoretical and empirical properties of various election procedures. Recently, a sub-literature on rationalizability, led by social choice theorists and computer scientists, makes explicit connections between social choice rules and maximum likelihood estimation. This paper further illuminates connections between social choice and statistical summaries of data. We begin by studying rationalizability from a statistical perspective, expanding existing results to realistic voting contexts and deriving statistical details necessary for model estimation and assessment. We then apply our work to ranked-choice votes from the 2025 New York City Democratic mayoral primary election. We demonstrate how rationalizing models impose unrealistic distributional assumptions and thus exhibit poor fit to voting data. Additionally, we show that non-rationalizing models meaningfully elucidate heterogeneous voter preferences. Our work demonstrates the inability of rationalizing models to capture key features of distributions of preferences in political elections, depriving analysts of fundamental uses, such as inference, typically associated with statistical modeling. Conversely, we show that statistical modeling can capture nuanced voter preferences by paying careful attention to plausible data-generating mechanisms.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Tensor-Train Methods for 3D Linear Elasticity: Block and Global Operator Representations with Solver Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23595)
*Quoc Thai Tran,Duc P. Truong,William Dai,Kim Ø. Rasmussen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于张量列（TT）格式的等几何分析方法，用于大规模三维线弹性问题求解。通过利用基函数的张量积结构和几何相关系数场的低秩特性，将刚度算子、质量算子、力向量及位移解均表示为TT格式，并分别提出了分块算子与单一块算子两种求解策略，数值实验表明相比传统稀疏满网格表示实现了显著的压缩。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模三维线弹性问题在等几何分析框架下计算代价极高，传统稀疏满网格存储和求解方式面临内存与计算瓶颈。作者旨在利用问题中固有的张量积结构和低秩特征来打破这一瓶颈，实现高效可扩展的大规模仿真。

Method: 1) 将等几何分析中的刚度算子、质量算子、力向量和位移解统一表示为TT格式，利用张量积基函数与低秩系数场结构；2) 提出两种求解策略：分块算子公式（耦合弹性算子存为分离的TT块）+ 无矩阵三场TT共轭梯度求解器；以及单算子公式（完整耦合系统存为单一整体TT算子）+ AMEn求解器。

Result: 数值实验表明，与传统稀疏满网格表示相比，TT格式在算子存储和位移解两方面均实现了显著的压缩，验证了TT公式在三维大规模弹性仿真中的高效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: TT格式结合等几何分析为大规模三维线弹性问题提供了一种高效、可扩展的求解路径，能够在显著降低存储与计算开销的同时保持数值精度，为更大规模的真实工程仿真奠定了基础。

Abstract: This work develops tensor-train (TT) formulations for solving large-scale three-dimensional linear elasticity problems discretized by isogeometric analysis. By exploiting the tensor-product structure of the basis functions and the low-rank structure of geometry-dependent coefficient fields, the stiffness operator, mass operator, force vector, and displacement solution are represented in TT format. Two solution strategies are investigated: a block-operator formulation, in which the coupled elasticity operator is stored as separated TT blocks, and a single-operator formulation, in which the full coupled system is stored as one monolithic TT operator. A matrix-free three-field TT conjugate-gradient solver is introduced for the block formulation, while AMEn is used for the single-operator formulation. Numerical examples demonstrate substantial compression of both operators and solutions compared with conventional sparse full-grid representations, showing that TT-based formulations provide an efficient and scalable approach for large-scale three-dimensional elasticity simulations.

</details>


### [90] [Algorithmic Cost in "Exact Real Computation"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23603)
*Jihoon Hyun,Holger Thies,Martin Ziegler*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对精确实数计算（ERC）编程语言进行了定量分析，通过为ERC的运算原语分配位代价，证明了ERC与图灵机在多项式时间等价意义上的强Church-Turing完备性，并在iRRAM C++库中进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的工作仅证明了ERC与传统可计算分析中图灵机在定性层面（图灵完备性）的等价，但缺乏定量的复杂性分析。为了将这种等价性从定性提升到多项式时间层面的定量等价，需要在ERC的运算原语上引入合理的代价模型，从而明确ERC的计算复杂度特征。

Method: 为ERC的运算原语（包括偏序测试和多值测试）分配位代价（bit-cost），建立代价模型，使得任意在多项式代价下可计算的实值函数在多项式时间内图灵可计算，反之亦然。同时在iRRAM C++库的实现了上进行了运行时测量以验证理论性能预测。

Result: 证明了ERC在多项式时间层面与图灵机定量等价，即强Church-Turing假设意义上的完备性；iRRAM C++库上的运行时实测结果与理论性能预测一致。

Conclusion: ERC不仅在图灵完备性（定性）层面与可计算分析中的图灵机等价，而且在多项式时间复杂性（定量）层面也实现了强Church-Turing等价，为ERC作为实际数值计算工具的复杂度可靠性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Turing completeness of a programming language or system characterizes its expressive power; and the strong Church-Turing hypo-/thesis refines such from qualitative to polynomial-time equivalence. Exact Real Computation (ERC) is a novel numerical programming language paradigm: for the imperative processing of continuous data as entities appearing as exact, i.e. devoid of rounding errors [doi:10.1007/978-3-662-44199-2_107]. ERC has been designed [doi:10.46298/lmcs-20(2:17)2024] as convenient and practical alternative, namely proven qualitatively equivalent, to the Turing machines originally underlying Computable Analysis [doi:10.1007/978-3-642-56999-9,doi:10.1007/978-1-4684-6802-1].
  The present work quantitatively strengthens this qualitative Turing-completeness: We assign bit-costs to ERC's operational primitives (including partial/multivalued tests) in such a way that any real function incurring polynomial cost becomes Turing-computable in polynomial time, and vice versa. Runtime measurements on implementations in the iRRAM C++ library confirm our theoretical performance predictions.

</details>


### [91] [The least squares RBF-PU method for linear elasticity in the diaphragm geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23718)
*Andreas Michael,Elisabeth Larsson,Pierre-Frédéric Villard,Davoud Mirzaei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文利用非贴体最小二乘径向基函数分区单元法（unfitted least-squares RBF partition of unity method）对真实人体膈肌几何进行线弹性变形模拟，通过理论证明该方法在薄几何域上具有高阶收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体膈肌对呼吸至关重要，对其数值模拟有助于理解机械通气患者出现的并发症。然而膈肌几何非常薄，这对大多数数值方法构成了挑战，亟需一种适合薄几何域模拟的数值方法。

Method: 采用非贴体最小二乘径向基函数分区单元法，该方法利用其非贴体特性以及圆柱形贴片的形状与细化策略来处理薄几何域问题。将真实膈肌几何建模为线弹性体，并施加实际边界条件进行变形计算。

Result: 推导了理论证明，表明该方法对该问题以高阶精度收敛到精确解。

Abstract: We develop a Monte Carlo lattice Boltzmann method to efficiently compute statistical solutions to the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes and Euler equations. By employing entropic space-time adaptive relaxation for higher order kinetic moments, our scheme is found to yield stable and consistent numerical solutions in the vanishing viscosity limit. We establish a convergence result that is conditional on four explicitly stated assumptions regarding the discrete dynamics. First, under diffusive scaling, the laws of the discrete ensemble are shown to converge along a subsequence to a limit satisfying the Foias--Temam Liouville formulation of the Navier--Stokes equations. Second, by lifting these measures to the Fjordholm--Mishra--Weber framework, we show that, provided the structure-function scaling holds uniformly, the vanishing viscosity limit satisfies the multi-point statistical Euler hierarchy. Unconditionally, we establish weak-strong uniqueness of the limit measures on the interval of existence of a strong Euler solution, and under explicit scaling assumptions, we formally derive a fractional 1-Wasserstein convergence rate of approximately $0.5$. Our method enables the first three-dimensional computations of time-dependent statistical solutions along the inviscid limit of the incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. Numerical experiments on a randomized Taylor--Green vortex with 24-dimensional initial uncertainty successfully recover Kolmogorov's K41 scaling for energy spectra and structure functions, exhibit the failure of pathwise strong convergence, and are consistent with the predicted Wasserstein convergence rate. Finally, based on error measurements with respect to spectral hyperviscosity computations, we provide numerical evidence for the universality of statistical solutions of the incompressible Euler equations.

<details>

Result: 该文给出了UWVF与PWDG等价的简洁结构证明，建立了两个互余的标准形式：PWDG坐标下为K+iI，UWVF坐标下为½I+iK_U，从而将经典等价性从"特定罚参数选取"重新诠释为同一边耦合问题在不同坐标系下的表示。

Conclusion: UWVF与PWDG的等价性本质上是断裂场坐标与阻抗迹坐标之间的坐标变换结果，这为理解两种方法的关系提供了更深刻的数学结构视角，也暗示了两种方法在离散层面的同构性。

Abstract: The ultra-weak variational formulation (UWVF) is known to be the Trefftz discontinuous Galerkin scheme obtained from the standard PWDG flux family by setting \(α=β=δ=1/2\). We give a short structural explanation of this fact. On every interior mesh edge, the two PWDG jump terms at these parameter values are exactly one half of the sum of the squared norms of the two oriented impedance-transmission defects used by the UWVF. Thus the Trefftz-DG norm is the natural Riesz norm of the global transmission defect. In PWDG coordinates its Gramian is the anti-Hermitian part of the discrete matrix, and Riesz normalization puts the operator in the normal form \(K+\ii I\), with \(K\) Hermitian. The same argument applied directly to the discrete UWVF yields the complementary normal form \(\tfrac12 I+\ii K_{\rm U}\). This identifies the classical equivalence as a relation between broken-field and impedance-trace coordinates for the same edge-coupling problem, rather than only as a particular choice of penalties.

</details>

Abstract: In this report, we recall Christiansen's [2011, Num. Math.] discrete elasticity complex that uses spaces of polynomials and distributions. Using the degrees of freedom of those spaces, we write a complex using vector-valued chains and co-chains that is isomorphic to Christiansen's complex. The operators of the new complex are algebraic, but use the geometry of the simplicial complex. Finally, we define a de Rham-like map from the smooth elasticity complex to this discrete complex.

</details>

Method: 提出了在DeC方法的连续迭代步骤之间引入插值过程的改进方案。该方法使得低阶迭代在更便宜的低阶离散结构上执行，从而降低计算开销。该修改赋予了方法天然的p自适应特性。作者针对两类DeC方法族进行了研究，并通过数值验证、效率评估和稳定性区域图进行系统分析。

Result: 数值实验证实了修改后的方案能在降低计算成本的同时提供高保真度的结果，展示了自适应策略的有效性。测试涵盖了刚性的ODEs以及具有高阶空间导数的偏微分方程(PDEs)等多个案例，稳定性区域图也验证了方法的稳定性特性。

Conclusion: 通过迭代间插值的修改赋予了IMEX DeC方法显著的效率和自适应性优势，使其在保证高精度结果的同时大幅降低计算开销，特别适用于涉及刚性问题和高阶空间导数PDE的实际应用场景。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate implicit-explicit (IMEX) arbitrary high-order Deferred Correction (DeC) methods for the approximation of ordinary differential equations (ODEs). Such schemes are characterized by an iterative procedure that increases the order of accuracy by one at each iteration. More precisely, we study an efficient modification based on the introduction of interpolation processes between consecutive iterations, with the aim of systematically matching the accuracy achieved at each iteration with the order of the discretization employed. On the one hand, this modification leads to computational advantages, since the low-order iterations are performed on cheaper lower-order discretization structures; on the other hand, it endows the methods with a natural $p$-adaptive character, which is particularly appealing in the context of practical applications. We investigate this modification for two families of DeC schemes, providing numerical validation, efficiency assessments, and stability region plots. The numerical validation includes several examples involving stiff ODEs and partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-order spatial derivatives. The ability of the modified schemes to provide high-fidelity results at reduced computational cost, as well as the effectiveness of the adaptive strategy, is demonstrated through the numerical experiments.

</details>

<details>

Method: 采用GTransNet进行空间逼近，使用稳定BDF格式进行时间离散，得到一阶和二阶时间精度的GTransNet-BDF格式。通过配点法实现，每个时间步求解一个常系数最小二乘系统。为解决该系统中非零最小二乘残差导致的质量不守恒问题，提出一种基于最小化问题的后处理质量守恒投影，其求解计算代价可忽略。

Result: 在二维和三维空间中进行了大量数值实验，验证了该方法在收敛性、质量守恒和能量耗散方面的表现，展示了GTransNet-BDF格式的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法具有预定义隐层和网格无关性的关键优势，适用于复杂计算域、变迁移率和长时间模拟等问题场景。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with a structure-preserving neural network-based framework for the Cahn-Hilliard equation in mixed form. We employ generalized transferable neural networks (GTransNet) for spatial approximation and stabilized backward differentiation formulas (BDF) for temporal discretization. The resulting first- and second-order in time GTransNet-BDF schemes are shown to conserve mass and satisfy energy stability at the time-discrete level. The schemes are implemented by a collocation-based method, in which a least-squares system with constant coefficient matrix needs to be solved at each time step. The solution of this system, which determines the output-layer weights of the network, violates mass conservation due to the expected nonzero least-squares residual. To overcome this issue, we introduce a novel post-processing mass-conserving projection that enforces the mass constraint through a minimization problem, whose solution can be computed at negligible computational cost. A key advantage of the proposed method lies in its predetermined hidden layers and mesh-free nature, making the method applicable to complex domains, variable mobility, and long-time simulations. Extensive numerical experiments in two and three dimensions verify convergence, mass conservation, and energy dissipation as well as demonstrate the accuracy and robustness of the proposed GTransNet-BDF schemes.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种在四边形和六面体网格上的节点等参浸入式有限元（IFE）方法，通过在选择离散通量条件的强制点来克服传统IFE基函数在张量扩散系数下不可唯一解（unisolvence）的问题，建立了最优误差估计并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 所提出的IFE方法具有以下优势：适用于复杂几何、无角度限制、可用于张量扩散系数，从而克服了现有矩形和三角形网格上节点IFE方法的局限。建立了最优误差估计，并通过数值实验加以确认。

Conclusion: 通过合理选择通量条件的强制点，可以彻底解决IFE基函数在张量扩散系数下的唯一可解性问题，使IFE方法在四边形和六面体网格上达到最优的逼近精度与灵活性。

Abstract: Immersed finite element (IFE) methods provide an effective framework for solving interface problems on unfitted meshes. The basic idea underlying IFE methods is to modify standard finite element spaces by enforcing interface conditions at certain points. It is known that, for nodal degrees of freedom, unisolvence of linear IFE basis functions on triangular elements is subject to a non-obtuse-angle condition for scalar diffusion coefficients. This limitation is more severe for tensor diffusion coefficients, for which the non-obtuse-angle condition is no longer sufficient for unisolvence. Even on rectangular meshes, conventional bilinear IFE basis functions may not be unisolvent for tensor diffusion coefficients. In this paper, we develop and analyze a nodal isoparametric IFE method on quadrilateral and hexahedral meshes and show that the unisolvence issue can be overcome by appropriately selecting the enforcement point of the discrete flux condition. The key observation is that, unlike triangular elements, the discrete flux in quadrilateral and hexahedral elements is not constant, which provides the flexibility to select such an enforcement point to ensure unisolvence. We provide a systematic procedure for this selection that not only ensures unisolvence of the IFE basis functions on general quadrilateral and hexahedral elements with either scalar or tensor-valued diffusion coefficients but also preserves the optimal approximation properties of the resulting IFE space. The proposed IFE method offers several advantages: flexibility for complex geometries, the absence of angle restrictions, and applicability to tensor diffusion coefficients, thereby overcoming the limitations of existing nodal IFE methods on rectangular and triangular meshes. Optimal error estimates are established and confirmed by numerical experiments.

</details>


### [98] [Derivative-Free Recovery of a Nonlinearity in a Free-Boundary DCIS Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24090)
*De-Han Chen,Hongyu Liu,Keji Liu*

TL;DR: 本文研究乳腺导管原位癌（DCIS）多维自由边界模型中的逆系数问题，从营养浓度的时间轨迹（针吸活检获得）中恢复未知的养分消耗函数。通过将逆问题转化为不动点问题，证明离散不动点的存在性及收敛性，并开发了结合Picard迭代与Sperner搜索的同伦延拓方法进行数值求解。


<details>

Motivation: DCIS是一种早期乳腺癌，其病变进展由肿瘤界面动力学驱动，涉及营养浓度、组织压力和曲率的非线性耦合。在实际临床中，通过针吸活检获取营养浓度的时间轨迹是可行的，但如何从这些观测数据中反演养分消耗函数（反映肿瘤代谢活性的关键参数）在数学上具有重要理论和应用价值。

Method: (1) 正问题：在允许的消耗函数类上建立一致局部适定性；(2) 将逆问题重述为不动点问题，用有限维空间逼近允许集并构造离散迭代算子；(3) 证明离散不动点的存在性以及子列的强收敛性到连续算子的不动点；(4) 提出同伦延拓方法，结合线性收敛的Picard迭代与立方体Sperner搜索，无需对目标泛函求导或计算伴随状态。

Result: 理论上证明了离散不动点的存在性及其子序列强收敛到连续算子不动点（该不动点在一致性条件下求解逆问题）；数值实验在径向对称和非对称DCIS模型上验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为DCIS逆系数问题提供了严格的理论框架和无需梯度/伴随的数值求解策略，在数学理论与数值实验两方面均证实了方法的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: We investigate an inverse coefficient problem for a multidimensional free-boundary model of ductal carcinoma in situ (DCIS), in which the tumor interface is governed by the nonlinear coupling of nutrient concentration, tissue pressure and curvature, and the unknown nutrient consumption function is recovered from a temporal trace of the nutrient concentration obtained by needle aspiration biopsy. For the forward problem, we establish uniform local well-posedness over an admissible class of consumption functions. The inverse problem is recast as a fixed-point problem: approximating the admissible set by finite-dimensional spaces yields discrete iteration operators, for which we prove the existence of fixed points, and the strong convergence of a subsequence of discrete fixed points to a fixed point of the continuous operator, which solves the inverse problem under a consistency condition. To approximate these fixed points, we develop a homotopy-continuation method combining a linearly convergent Picard iteration with a cubical Sperner search, without differentiating an objective functional or computing an adjoint state. Several numerical experiments on radially symmetric and non-symmetric DCIS models corroborate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [99] [Strang's Conjecture: Positive Result on Strong Collapsible Complexes and a Code to Check the Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24120)
*Aranzazu Romero,Renaldi-Bradley Bodombo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究Strang猜想，该猜想预测无孔多边形区域上二元C^1样条空间的维数。作者将样条空间与相关函数空间相关联，利用内在算子构造复形，获得与Strang猜想等价的表述，并提出NGSolve代码进行计算验证。此外，证明了若三角剖分对应的单纯复形是强可坍缩的（满足非共线条件），则k=2时Strang猜想成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样条在计算机辅助几何设计、代数几何和有限元方法等领域有广泛应用，但其空间维数的确定仍是一个开放问题。Strang猜想对k=4和k≥5的情形已被证明，但对k=2的情形John Morgan和L. Ridgway Scott提供了反例。因此需要更深入的理论框架来理解和验证该猜想。

Method: 作者首先将二元C^1样条空间（度≤k）与其他函数空间建立联系，在这些空间上利用内在算子构造一个复形，从而得到Strang猜想的等价命题。该等价命题可通过计算方式验证猜想的成立性，作者据此提供NGSolve代码。此外，作者证明若三角剖分对应的单纯复形是强可坍缩的（附加非共线条件），则k=2时Strang猜想成立。

Result: 获得了Strang猜想的等价表述以及相应的计算验证框架（NGSolve代码），并证明了在强可坍缩（非共线条件）的三角剖分上，k=2情形下Strang猜想成立。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种新的理论框架将Strang猜想转化为可计算的等价形式，并通过强可坍缩条件在k=2情形下给出了一类保证猜想成立的充分条件，为最终解决Strang猜想提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Splines, which are piecewise polynomial functions with given smoothness, are used in numerous applications such as computer aided geometric design, algebraic geometry and for implementation of the finite element method. In many applications, it is important to know the dimension of the space of splines over a domain, but this continues to be an open problem. Strang's conjecture predicts the dimension of the space of bivariate $C^1$ splines of degree at most $k$ over a polygonal domain with no holes. For the cases, $k=4$ and $k \geq 5$ the conjecture has been proven true. For the case $k=2$, John Morgan and L. Ridgway Scott provided a counterexample. In this project, we begin by relating the space of bivariate $C^1$ splines of degree at most $k$ to various other function spaces. With the aid of some intrinsic operators on these spaces, we obtain a complex that yields an equivalence statement to Strang's conjecture. The equivalent statement admits a computational approach to the conjecture and thus we present an NGSolve code to check Strang's conjecture for a given mesh. In addition, we prove that if the simplicial complex associated to a triangulation is strongly collapsible (with a non-collinear condition), then Strang's conjecture holds for $k=2$.

</details>


### [100] [Condition numbers of block Toeplitz matrices and stability of space-time IgA approximations for the wave and Schrödinger equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24151)
*Manuel Bogoya,Albrecht Böttcher,Matteo Ferrari,Sergei M Grudsky,Stefano Serra-Capizzano*

TL;DR: 本文研究了分块Toeplitz矩阵条件数在矩阵尺寸趋于无穷时的增长行为，将其从标量情形推广到固定分块大小N的分块情形，并将理论应用于时空Galerkin方法中Isogeometric离散的稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有工作研究了带状Toeplitz矩阵条件数随矩阵尺寸增长的行为，但仅限于标量情形。本文将这一研究扩展到分块Toeplitz矩阵，并进一步将其应用于时空Galerkin方法的稳定性分析，填补了分块情形下的理论空白。

Result: 给出了分块Toeplitz矩阵条件数的上下界，并通过例子展示条件数可能以任意快的速度增长。在应用层面，将理论用于时空Galerkin方法的稳定性分析，对具体例子进行了详细处理并给出了相关数值实验及批判性讨论。最后列出若干相关的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文完整分析了分块Toeplitz矩阵条件数的渐近增长行为，并成功将其应用于时空Isogeometric Galerkin方法的稳定性分析，为相关数值方法的理论分析提供了有力工具，同时指出了未来研究的方向和开放问题。

Abstract: In previous work by several authors, the behavior of the condition numbers of banded Toeplitz matrices was studied as the matrix size tends to infinity. In the present contribution, two main directions are pursued. As a first step, we extend this study to block Toeplitz matrices with blocks of fixed size $N$. As in the scalar case, we show that even when the symbol generates a Fredholm infinite Toeplitz operator, the condition numbers of the finite matrices may grow at least exponentially. Upper and lower bounds for the condition numbers are obtained, and examples showing that they may grow arbitrarily fast are presented. Then, as a second step, we apply the developed theory to the stability analysis of space-time Galerkin methods, where in time an Isogeometric approach is used with regularity $r$, $1\le r\le p-1$, $p$ being the employed polynomial degree. These stability issues are related exactly to the conditioning of block Toeplitz-like matrices with blocks of size $N=p-r$. Specific examples are treated in detail and related numerical experiments are presented and critically discussed. We finally present a short list of relevant open problems.

</details>


### [101] [Stability, Convergence, and Error Analysis of Finite Element Methods for 3D Magnetohydrodynamics with $p$-Laplacian Viscosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24197)
*Kim Ngan Le,Akash Ashirbad Panda,Evana Islam Sarkar*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可压缩MHD系统的有限元分析主要局限于牛顿流体（p=2），而非牛顿流体的p-Laplace粘性在三维MHD问题中的数值分析与收敛性证明仍是开放问题。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立适用于剪切增稠流体（p>2）的全离散有限元框架，并推广到经典牛顿情形。

Method: 采用空间有限元离散与半隐式Euler时间格式相结合的全离散方案。收敛性证明依赖于两个关键技术：一是时间平移紧致性论证（time-translation compactness）以处理非线性项和弱解提取；二是Minty单调性方法以处理p-Laplace算子带来的非线性。误差分析在额外正则性假设下进行，且不设时间步长与网格尺寸之间的耦合限制。

Result: 证明了所提格式收敛到弱解；在正则性假设下获得了速度场和磁场场的无条件误差估计（时间步长与网格尺寸解耦）；数值实验验证了理论收敛阶并展示了方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将不可压缩MHD系统的有限元分析从牛顿情形（p=2）推广至非牛顿剪切增稠流（p>2），并为该问题提供了收敛性和误差分析的理论框架，具有理论与算法双重贡献。

Abstract: This paper develops a fully discrete finite element method for three-dimensional incompressible magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) flows with nonlinear $p$-Laplace viscosity. The scheme combines spatial finite elements with a semi-implicit Euler time discretisation. Convergence to a weak solution is proved using a time-translation compactness argument together with Minty's monotonicity method. Under additional regularity assumptions, we derive unconditional error estimates for both the velocity and magnetic field, with no coupling restriction between the time step and mesh size. The framework extends finite element analysis of incompressible MHD systems to non-Newtonian shear-thickening fluids ($p>2$), while recovering the classical Newtonian case ($p=2$). Finally, numerical simulations are provided to validate the theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate the robustness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [102] [Eigenvalue bounds for preconditioned symmetric multiple saddle-point matrices with block-triangular preconditioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24203)
*Luca Bergamaschi,Michele Bergamaschi,John W. Pearson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对对称分块三对角多重鞍点线性系统，在基于近似Schur补的分块三角预条件子下，证明了所有具有非平凡虚部的复特征值严格包含在以1为圆心的复平面圆内，并将实正特征值用参数多项式序列的极值根界定。数值实验验证了边界的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重鞍点系统出现在许多科学与工程应用中（如约束优化、流体力学等），其高效求解依赖于预条件子的设计。已有工作多针对特定块数的鞍点系统，缺乏对任意块数、在近似Schur补预条件子下特征值谱分布的统一定量分析。

Method: 基于分块三对角结构和近似Schur补构造分块三角预条件子；利用特征值分析技术将复特征值的虚部约束在一个以1为圆心的圆内，并将实特征值通过构造参数多项式序列，借助其极值根建立上下界。

Result: 证明了任意块数下所有非实特征值严格落入以1为圆心的复平面圆内；实正特征值被参数多项式极值根所界定；数值实验表明所推导的边界与预条件矩阵的实际特征值分布吻合良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的特征值界为含多个块的多重鞍点系统的预条件迭代求解提供了可靠的谱分析工具，对预条件子的有效性与收敛性分析具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: We develop eigenvalue bounds for symmetric, block-tridiagonal multiple saddle-point linear systems, preconditioned with block-triangular matrices, based on approximate Schur complements. Irrespective on the number of blocks, we prove that all complex eigenvalues, with nontrivial imaginary part, are strictly contained in a circle within the complex plane, with center 1. The real and positive eigenvalues are bounded in terms of the extremal roots of a sequences of parametric polynomials. Numerical results reveal that the bounds describe very well the eigenvalue distribution of the preconditioned matrix.

</details>


### [103] [Robust training and rigorous error analysis of physics-informed neural networks for the $p$-Laplace equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24205)
*Kyueon Choi,Seungchan Ko,Dohyun Kwon*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对物理信息神经网络（PINNs）求解非线性偏微分方程提出了一种鲁棒的训练框架与严格的误差分析，具体针对p-Laplace方程设计了结合对偶残差损失与分数阶Sobolev范数边界损失的新型损失函数，并建立了先验与后验误差估计，同时扩展到参数化设置并以数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管PINNs在众多问题中广泛应用，但大多数理论分析局限于线性方程，对于非线性问题，PINNs与传统数值分析之间存在显著差距。作者旨在通过提出新的损失函数框架和严格的误差分析来弥合这一理论空缺。

Method: 针对p-Laplace方程，作者提出一种新的损失公式，包含两部分：(1) 在W^{-1,p'}空间中度量的对偶残差损失；(2) 在分数阶Sobolev范数W^{1-1/p,p}中度量的边界损失。该损失设计考虑了弱解的有限正则性，从而能够建立严格的先验和后验误差估计。此外，该框架被推广到参数化场景，其中指数、源项和边界条件均可随参数变化。

Result: 建立了针对非线性p-Laplace方程的严格先验和后验误差估计，证明所提出的损失函数框架能够有效应对弱解的有限正则性问题；同时通过数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种适用于非线性PDE的PINNs鲁棒训练框架，填补了PINNs在非线性问题上缺乏严格理论分析的空白，并通过理论证明与数值实验双重验证了方法的有效性与可靠性。

Abstract: With the rise of scientific machine learning, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have been extensively applied to a wide range of problems. Nevertheless, most theoretical analyses of PINNs remain confined to linear equations, and a substantial gap persists between PINNs and classical numerical analysis for nonlinear problems. To address this issue, we propose a robust training framework for PINNs solving nonlinear partial differential equations, together with a rigorous error analysis. Specifically, for the $p$-Laplace equation, we introduce a novel loss formulation that combines a dual residual loss measured in $W^{-1,p'}$ with a boundary loss measured in a fractional Sobolev norm $W^{1-\frac{1}{p},p}$. This formulation is designed to accommodate the limited regularity of weak solutions and enables us to establish rigorous \textit{a priori} and \textit{a posteriori} error estimates. Moreover, the proposed framework and its analysis are extended to a parametric setting in which the exponent, the source term, and the boundary condition may all vary with the parameters. Finally, we present numerical experiments that substantiate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [104] [Samplet compression for conditionally positive definite kernels and universal Kriging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24283)
*Sara Avesani,Rüdiger Kempf,Michael Multerer,Holger Wendland*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于samplet（样本小波）的框架，用于高效数值求解由条件正定(CPD)核函数逼近（特别是通用克里金）产生的鞍点系统。利用samplet的消失矩性质和对应的离散正交多项式缩放分布结构，将不定鞍点系统自然降维为一个大线性系统（细节系数）加一个小三角系统（多项式系数），实现O(N log N)的组装和存储复杂度，并通过稀疏Cholesky分解高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPD核函数逼近和通用克里金中产生的鞍点系统通常是不定的大规模线性系统，传统直接或迭代求解方法计算代价高。需要一种数值高效的表示和求解框架来降低复杂度，特别是在大规模三维问题中。

Method: 基于samplet框架：利用samplet的消失矩性质以及对应于离散正交多项式的缩放分布结构，实现鞍点系统的天然零空间约简——将不定鞍点系统转化为一个大型线性系统（用于细节系数）和一个小型三角系统（用于多项式系数）。推导了多调和样条在Beppo-Levi空间中的误差界，证明细节系数恰好张成CPD核正定的子空间，使约简后的分块矩阵对称正定。利用渐近光滑核的samplet变换核矩阵的拟稀疏性，实现O(N log N)的组装与存储，配合稀疏Cholesky分解高效求解。

Result: 该框架实现了O(N log N)的计算和存储复杂度，约简系统为对称正定矩阵可用稀疏Cholesky高效求解。在三个应用（广义协方差的Gaussian过程回归、基于samplet压缩薄板样条的 landmark 图像配准、三维网格变形）中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: samplet框架为CPD核逼近和通用克里金中的鞍点系统提供了一种高效、低复杂度的数值求解方案，兼具理论保证（误差界和正定性证明）和广泛的实际应用前景。

Abstract: We present a samplet-based framework for the efficient numerical solution of saddle-point systems arising from conditionally positive definite (CPD) kernel approximation in general and universal Kriging in particular. The vanishing moment property of samplets as well as the particular structure of the associated scaling distributions, which correspond to discrete orthogonal polynomials, allow for a numerically favorable representation of these saddle-point systems. Concretely, they enable a natural null-space reduction of the indefinite saddle-point system to a (large) linear system for the detail coefficients and a small triangular system for the polynomial coefficients. We derive error bounds for the approximation by polyharmonic splines in Beppo-Levi spaces and show that the detail coefficients span precisely the subspace on which the CPD kernel is positive definite, rendering the reduced block symmetric positive definite. In view of the quasi-sparsity of the samplet-transformed kernel matrix for asymptotically smooth kernels, the resulting method achieves O(N log N) cost for the assembly and the storage of the saddle-point system. The reduced system can efficiently be solved by a sparse Cholesky factorization. We illustrate the framework with three applications, namely Gaussian process regression with generalized covariances, landmark-based image registration via samplet-compressed thin plate splines, and three-dimensional mesh deformation.

</details>


### [105] [A High-Accuracy Numerical Homogenization Framework for Quasiperiodic Hamilton--Jacobi Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24313)
*Kai Jiang,Meng Li,Juan Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对准周期Hamilton-Jacobi方程（QHJEs）提出了一套精确的数值均匀化框架。作者引入了准周期边界条件将全空间问题转化到有界域，并提出了结合半拉格朗日近似与有限点恢复方法的SL-FPR方案，建立了稳定性与误差估计，并将均匀化结果从二次情形推广到一般k>1的凸Hamilton量情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算准周期设置下的有效Hamilton量需要求解定义在全空间上的QHJEs，其解既不具有平移对称性也不衰减，且可能具有低正则性，这些特征给数值计算带来了巨大挑战，亟需开发有效的数值方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 引入准周期边界条件，将原全空间问题在有界域上处理并保持边界处的准周期性；2) 提出SL-FPR方案，结合半拉格朗日（semi-Lagrangian）近似与有限点恢复（finite points recovery）方法；3) 建立该格式的稳定性与误差估计；4) 将准周期均匀化结果从二次情形推广到一般k>1的情形。

Result: 数值实验展示了该方法在有效Hamilton量近似计算中的收敛性与适用性，验证了推广后的均匀化结果；同时建立了SL-FPR格式的稳定性与误差估计，证明了方法的理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了适用于一般凸Hamilton量（k>1）的准周期QHJEs数值均匀化框架，通过SL-FPR方案与准周期边界条件的结合，有效克服了全空间问题带来的数值困难，为该类问题的数值求解提供了可靠且理论支撑完备的工具。

Abstract: In this work, we develop an accurate numerical homogenization framework for computing effective Hamiltonians of quasiperiodic Hamilton--Jacobi equations (QHJEs) with convex Hamiltonians of the form $H(x,p) = |p|^k/{k}-f(x), ~k>1$, where $f$ is quasiperiodic. Computing effective Hamiltonians in the quasiperiodic setting requires solving QHJEs posed on the whole space. Their solutions generally possess neither translational symmetry nor decay and may exhibit low regularity. These features pose substantial challenges for numerical computation. To address these difficulties, we introduce a quasiperiodic boundary condition, which allows the original whole-space problem to be treated on a bounded domain while preserving quasiperiodicity at the boundary. We then propose an SL--FPR scheme that combines a semi-Lagrangian approximation with the finite points recovery method and establish stability and error estimates for the resulting scheme. We also extend the quasiperiodic homogenization result from the quadratic case to general $k>1$ and apply the proposed method to accurately approximate the corresponding effective Hamiltonians. Numerical experiments illustrate the convergence and applicability of the method and validate the extended homogenization results.

</details>


### [106] [Randomized Block Davidson Eigensolvers for Plane-Wave Density-Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24529)
*Moritz Gubler,Taejun Park,Augustin Bussy,Johannes Laute,Nicola Marzari,Iurii Timrov,Laura Grigori*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种随机化块Davidson型特征值求解器，用草图内积中的随机化Gram-Schmidt替代传统欧几里得正交化，仅需对基向量单次遍历且保持条件数界独立于输入向量。该方法在CPU和GPU上用混合精度实现，集成于DFTK中，在稀疏测试问题上当矩阵维度超过约2×10^4时超越确定性求解器，在5×10^5维度时快25%；但在完整SCF DFT计算中，两种Davidson变体仅比LOBPCG快5-11%，草图化的额外收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面波密度泛函理论（DFT）中，迭代对角化是主导计算成本，其中搜索空间正交化随问题规模和目标态数量的增加而快速增长，成为计算瓶颈。作者旨在通过随机化方法降低正交化的计算开销，同时保持求解器的收敛性和数值性质。

Method: 提出随机化块Davidson型特征值求解器，核心是用草图内积下的随机化Gram-Schmidt替代欧几里得正交化，仅需单次遍历基向量。该方法仅修改Rayleigh-Ritz步骤，使其变为定值广义Hermitian特征值问题，Ritz提取仍是精确的，保留了真实Ritz对和交错性质。实现上使用Julia单代码在CPU和GPU上以混合精度运行，与DFTK以矩阵自由方式接口，并在开源RandESC库中发布。

Result: 在稀疏测试问题中，当矩阵维度超过约2×10^4时，草图化求解器超越确定性对应版本，在5×10^5维度时快25%。在完整自洽场DFT计算中，两种Davidson变体仅比LOBPCG参考快5%至11%，且草图化的额外好处有限。

Conclusion: 草图化策略的收益取决于所需态的数量与问题维度的相对增长比例，而非特征值求解器本身。当所需态数随系统规模增长时，正交化节省被广义特征值问题的开销抵消。因此，草图化求解器的适用场景由所需态数的缩放行为决定。

Abstract: Iterative diagonalization is the dominant cost of plane-wave density-functional theory (DFT), with search-space orthogonalization scaling particularly quickly with problem size and the number of target states. We present a randomized block Davidson-type eigensolver that replaces Euclidean orthogonalization with randomized Gram-Schmidt in a sketched inner product, requiring only a single pass over the basis while keeping its conditioning bounded independently of the input vectors. This modification changes only the Rayleigh-Ritz step, which becomes a definite generalized Hermitian eigenproblem. Ritz extraction remains exact, preserving true Ritz pairs and the interlacing property that makes each band energy an upper bound on the true one. The method is implemented in mixed precision for CPUs and GPUs from a single Julia code, interfaces matrix-free with DFTK, and is released in the open-source RandESC library. On sparse test problems with a fixed number of eigenpairs, the sketched solver overtakes its deterministic counterpart beyond matrix dimensions of about $2\times 10^4$ and is $25\%$ faster at $5\times 10^5$. In full self-consistent field DFT calculations, however, both Davidson variants outperform the locally optimal block preconditioned conjugate gradient (LOBPCG) reference only by $5$ to $11\%$ in total time, while the additional benefit of sketching is limited. As the number of requested states grows with system size, orthogonalization savings are offset by the generalized eigenproblem. Therefore, the regime in which sketching pays off is set by how the number of wanted states scales with the problem dimension, not by the eigensolver as such.

</details>


### [107] [Efficient Hermitian and skew-Hermitian splitting methods for linear systems in micromagnetic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24576)
*Yingxi Miao,Changjian Xie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Landau-Lifshitz方程的半隐式离散线性系统，提出了使用Hermitian/skew-Hermitian splitting (HSS)方法及其非精确变体(IHSS)来高效求解。数值实验表明HSS迭代的谱半径低于理论界且严格小于1，IHSS方法收敛性良好但对分裂参数敏感，两种半隐式格式表现出相似的收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landau-Lifshitz方程的半隐式方法产生的离散线性系统是大规模稀疏、非Hermitian但正定的系数矩阵系统，需要高效的迭代求解方法。传统直接解法或现有迭代方法在处理此类问题时可能效率低下，因此需要探索适用于这类特定结构线性系统的求解策略。

Method: 论文采用HSS（Hermitian/skew-Hermitian splitting）迭代方法及其非精确变体IHSS来求解半隐式离散得到的非Hermitian正定线性系统。HSS方法将系数矩阵分解为Hermitian部分和skew-Hermitian部分，分别进行求解；IHSS则通过非精确内迭代降低计算成本。通过一维和三维数值实验验证了收敛性及谱半径的理论上界。

Result: 数值实验表明：在测试的网格分辨率和阻尼参数下，HSS迭代谱半径始终低于理论上界且严格小于1；理论上界能紧密贴合实际谱半径，对收敛行为具有准确估计能力。IHSS方法在测试案例中表现出有效收敛，但其效率对分裂参数敏感。两种半隐式方案的收敛行为总体相当。

Conclusion: HSS及其非精确变体IHSS是求解Landau-Lifshitz方程半隐式离散线性系统的有效方法。理论上界为实际收敛行为提供了可靠的预测，IHSS的分裂参数需要精心选择以保证效率。

Abstract: For the Landau-Lifshitz equation, the discrete linear systems obtained by our semi-implicit method possess the following properties: they are large-sparse systems with non-Hermitian yet positive-definite coefficient matrices. To solve these systems efficiently, we apply the Hermitian/skew-Hermitian splitting (HSS) method and its inexact variant (IHSS). Numerical experiments in one and three dimensions show that the spectral radius of the HSS iteration remains below its theoretical upper bound and strictly below one for the tested grid resolutions and damping parameters. Moreover, the theoretical bound closely follows the actual spectral radius, providing an accurate estimate of the convergence behavior. The IHSS results demonstrate effective convergence for the tested cases and show that its efficiency is sensitive to the splitting parameter. Overall, the two semi-implicit schemes exhibit comparable convergence behavior.

</details>


### [108] [Real-time inverse solutions via neural matrix operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24833)
*Julie Pham,Thomas O'Leary-Roseberry,Omar Ghattas,Karen Willcox*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NEMO（NEural Matrix Operator）的降基神经算子方法，用于数字孪生场景下的实时数据同化。该方法针对由PDE控制、对模型参数m非线性依赖但对反演参数q线性依赖的时空动力学问题，通过神经算子近似从m到参数到观测算子F(m)的非线性映射，从而在新给定m时实现q的闭式反演求解，在保证反演精度的同时实现了超过三个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生需要实时预测和控制，但许多物理系统的数据同化本质上是受物理约束的反问题，使用传统PDE求解器在实时场景下计算不可行。因此亟需一种能够实时求解反问题的高效方法。

Method: NEMO利用问题的特殊物理结构——对模型参数m非线性、对反演参数q线性——构建神经算子来近似从m到参数到观测算子F(m)的非线性映射，输出为矩阵形式。在降维子空间中学习该映射后，对于新的m，可以直接闭式求解q的反问题，避免了在线调用PDE求解器。同时利用降基子空间实现实时不确定性量化。

Result: 在污染物输运初始条件识别和高超声速飞行器载荷识别两个实际应用中，NEMO相比用PDE求解器构造降阶算子实现了超过三个数量级的加速；与最先进的多输入神经算子相比，反演性能相当，但在线计算复杂度降低了一个数量级以上，并提供了实时不确定性量化能力。

Conclusion: NEMO通过利用PDE反问题的特殊结构（对m非线性、对q线性），成功实现了数字孪生场景下实时高精度数据同化，兼顾了反演质量、计算效率和不确定性量化，是面向实时物理反问题求解的有效方案。

Abstract: Rapid data assimilation is required for real-time prediction and control in digital twins. For many physical systems, the data assimilation task requires the solution of a physics-constrained inverse problem, which is often computationally intractable in real time using traditional physics solvers. This work presents a reduced-basis neural operator approach to enable real-time inverse problem solutions in the digital twin setting. Our approach specifically targets the large class of problems with spatiotemporal dynamics governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) that are parameterized nonlinearly with respect to model parameters $m$, and linearly with respect to inversion parameters $q$. Based on this physical structure, our neural operator approximates the nonlinear map from the model parameters $m$ to the parameter-to-observable operator $\mathcal{F}(m)$ in a reduced subspace. Since the output of the neural operator is the parameter-to-observable operator itself (manifested as a matrix), we refer to this approach as NEural Matrix Operator (NEMO). With NEMO, for new given $m$, we enable a closed-form inverse problem solution for $q$ in a reduced subspace. We apply NEMO in two real-world applications: contaminant transport initial condition identification, and hypersonic vehicle load identification. We show that NEMO delivers high quality inverse problem solutions for data assimilation in real time, with over three orders of magnitude speedup compared to constructing the reduced operator with the PDE solver. Further, NEMO demonstrates comparable inverse performance to a state-of-the-art multiple-input neural operator, while reducing online computational complexity by over an order of magnitude and providing real-time uncertainty quantification.

</details>

### [109] [On Angle-optimization and Simplification of Degree-1 Homology Representatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23949)
*Emerson G. Escolar,Yuta Shimada*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的拓扑数据分析中同调类最优代表元的选取方法，以环的总绝对曲率作为代价函数，通过角度惩罚偏离平面性、凸性和简单性的环，并将角度最优同调环问题转化为二元二次优化问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持久同调分析中，提取同调类的"最优"代表元对于识别感兴趣几何区域至关重要。此前的最优性定义基于最小化长度或体积，本文希望引入一种新的代价函数——总绝对曲率，以更有效地刻画代表元的几何特性。

Method: 将总绝对曲率定义为基于环边之间角度的代价函数，该函数会惩罚偏离平面性、凸性和简单性的环；将"角度最优同调环问题"重新表述为一个二元二次优化问题，并在人工合成数据上进行了实验验证。

Result: 实验在人工玩具数据上展示了所提出方法的效果，验证了总绝对曲率作为代价函数在同调类代表元优化中的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文为持久同调中的代表元优化问题提供了基于角度（曲率）的新视角，区别于传统的最短长度或最小体积准则，为后续在更复杂数据上的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: In topological data analysis, in particular persistent homology analysis, extracting "optimal" representatives for homology classes is crucial for identifying geometric regions of interest. In prior work, optimality is defined in terms of minimizing length or volume. In this work, we restrict our attention to a single homology class in degree $1$ and introduce the total absolute curvature of cycles as the cost function. We show that this cost function, based on angles between edges of cycles, penalizes departures from planarity, convexity, and simple-ness of the cycle representative. We formulate the "angle-optimal homologous cycle problem", recast it as a binary quadratic optimization problem, and show the results of experiments on artificial toy data.

</details>


### [110] [Computing an e-net of a closed hyperbolic surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24497)
*Vincent Delecroix,Vincent Despré,Camille Lanuel,Hugo Parlier,Monique Teillaud*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个从基本多边形表示出发在双曲曲面上构建ε-网的算法，基于Delaunay细化与边翻转维护三角剖分；针对短测地线周围任意长套圈导致ε-网尺寸无法仅由亏格限定的问题，引入"伪ε-网"概念，将曲面分解为ε-薄圆柱与厚部分的Delaunay三角剖分，并应用于长度谱与systole计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲曲面在数学及计算几何与拓扑中日益重要，高效算法的设计依赖受控复杂度的几何离散化，但此前缺乏从基本多边形表示出发构建ε-网的算法。同时，由于短测地线周围存在任意长的套圈，ε-网的尺寸不能仅由亏格界定，需要新的概念与方法克服这一困难。

Method: 提出基于Delaunay细化的ε-网构建算法，通过边翻转维护Delaunay三角剖分；引入伪ε-网概念，将双曲曲面分解为ε-薄圆柱（围绕短测地线）与厚部分的ε-网所覆盖的Delaunay三角剖分。

Result: 得到了计算ε-厚双曲曲面长度谱的算法，以及从伪log(√2)-网计算systole的算法。

Conclusion: 基于Delaunay的离散化方法为双曲曲面上的算法计算提供了实用且通用的框架，伪ε-网有效解决了短测地线带来的复杂性困难。

Abstract: Hyperbolic surfaces are a fundamental object in mathematics and play an increasingly important role in computational geometry and topology. A key ingredient in the design of efficient algorithms on such surfaces is the availability of a geometric discretization of controlled complexity. In this paper, we present the first algorithm for constructing e-nets on hyperbolic surfaces starting from a fundamental polygon representation. Our approach is based on Delaunay refinement and relies on maintaining Delaunay triangulations through edge flips.
  The size of an e-net cannot be bounded solely as a function of the genus because of the presence of arbitrarily long collars around short geodesics. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce the notion of a pseudo e-net, which decomposes the surface into e-thin cylinders together with a Delaunay triangulation over an e-net of the remaining thick part.
  As applications, we obtain algorithms for computing the length spectrum of an e-thick hyperbolic surface and for computing the systole from a pseudo log(sqrt(2))-net. These results demonstrate that Delaunay-based discretizations provide a practical and versatile framework for algorithmic computations on hyperbolic surfaces.

</details>
