### [111] [Global geometry of the genotype-phenotype map illuminates a trade-off between penetrance and mutational adaptability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24704)
*Yutaro Ikeda,Kunihiko Kaneko,Tetsuhiro S. Hatakeyama*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过一个受统计物理启发的抽象随机基因型-表型映射模型，穷举重建了全基因型空间中的表型分布及环境特异性适应度景观，揭示了高外显率基因型倾向于位于高适应度区域内部且突变稳健，而低外显率基因型则靠近区域边界并具有更高的突变可达性，从而产生外显率与突变适应性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究虽表明高外显率可能限制突变可达性（mutational access），但仅聚焦于进化后的基因型及其局部突变邻域，无法判断这种局部约束是否在环境变化下限制突变适应性。由于基因型和表型空间组合爆炸式增长，全面重构每个基因型的表型概率分布及环境特异适应度景观通常不可行，亟需一个可穷举分析的模型来解决这一根本性问题。

Method: 作者构建了一个受统计物理中相互作用自旋启发的抽象随机基因型-表型映射模型，使得映射的全空间穷举重构成为可能。通过分析高、低外显率基因型在环境特异高适应度区域中的全局几何位置（内部vs边界），结合进化模拟，考察固定环境下表型噪声强度和环境变化频率对选择压力的影响。

Result: 高外显率基因型位于环境特异高适应度区域内部且突变稳健，低外显率基因型位于边界附近并拥有更大的到达替代环境高适应度区域的突变通道。进化模拟显示：固定环境中更强的表型噪声增加可靠表达的选优优势，偏向高外显率和突变稳健性；频繁环境变化则偏向突变可达性而牺牲外显率。

Conclusion: 外显率与突变适应性是同一全局几何结构导致的两种对立后果，环境条件（表型噪声强度与环境变化频率）决定了二者的进化平衡。该工作为理解复杂基因型-表型映射中的多环境适应权衡提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Evolution in changing environments requires both reliable expression of the currently favored phenotype, as quantified by penetrance, and the capacity to reach alternative phenotypes through mutation. Previous studies suggest that high penetrance may restrict such mutational access. However, because these studies focus on evolved genotypes and local mutational neighborhoods, they cannot determine whether this local constraint limits mutational adaptability under environmental change. Such adaptability depends on a genotype's position relative to high-fitness regions for other environments. Addressing this question requires reconstructing the full probability distribution over phenotypes for every genotype and the resulting environment-specific fitness landscapes across genotype space. Such reconstruction is generally infeasible because genotype and phenotype spaces grow combinatorially. Here, an abstract model of stochastic genotype-phenotype mapping, inspired by interacting spins in statistical physics, permits exhaustive reconstruction of the map. We find that high-penetrance genotypes tend to occupy the interior of environment-specific high-fitness regions and are mutationally robust, whereas lower-penetrance genotypes tend to lie near their boundaries and have greater mutational access to high-fitness regions for alternative environments. This global geometry generates a trade-off between penetrance and mutational adaptability. In evolutionary simulations, stronger phenotypic noise in a fixed environment increases the selective advantage of reliable expression, thereby favoring high penetrance and mutational robustness. Frequent environmental change instead favors mutational accessibility at the expense of penetrance. Thus, penetrance and adaptability are opposing consequences of the same global geometry, with environmental conditions determining their evolutionary balance.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [On the Convergence Rate of the Duality Gap in the Generalized Frank-Wolfe Method Under the Weak Growth Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23767)
*Renbo Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析广义Frank-Wolfe方法中一系列对偶间隙的收敛速率，在Peña (2023)提出的弱增长条件的特定机制下，利用一个可能具有独立价值的递归引理进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frank-Wolfe方法是求解约束凸优化问题的重要一阶方法，其对偶间隙的收敛速率分析对于理解算法性能至关重要。本文希望通过在弱增长条件下分析对偶间隙序列的收敛速率，扩展对该方法的理论理解，并验证Peña (2023)所提条件的适用性。

Method: 本文的核心分析工具是一个递归引理（recursion lemma），该引理可能具有独立研究价值。作者在Peña (2023)提出的弱增长条件下，通过该递归引理对广义Frank-Wolfe方法中对偶间隙序列的收敛速率进行推导和证明。

Result: 在弱增长条件的特定机制下，证明了广义Frank-Wolfe方法中对偶间隙序列具有确定性的收敛速率，且该速率由递归引理精确刻画。

Conclusion: 广义Frank-Wolfe方法在弱增长条件下具有可量化的对偶间隙收敛速率，所提出的递归引理为后续相关收敛性分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We analyze the convergence rate of certain sequence of the duality gaps in the generalized Frank-Wolfe method, under certain regime of the weak growth condition proposed in Peña (2023). Our analysis leverages a recursion lemma that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [113] [Let AEDs Move: Urban Mobility Enhanced Defibrillator Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23810)
*Bahar Dehqani Viniche,Sheng Liu,Nooshin Salari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由城市网约车车队携带的移动式自动体外除颤器（AED）模型，在总AED供应量固定的前提下，比较了固定部署与移动部署（及混合策略）对院外心脏骤停（OHCA）响应时间与可靠性的影响，基于纽约和多伦多的真实数据验证了移动AED能显著改善响应，但最佳固定/移动配置比例因城市而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定式AED网络无法适应OHCA需求在时间和空间上的动态变化，导致及时获取AED仍然是公共除颤系统的主要瓶颈。作者希望通过利用现有的网约车车队携带AED，探索一种无需增加总AED容量即可提升急救响应能力的平台赋能方案。

Method: 在固定总AED供给的条件下，构建并比较纯固定部署、纯移动部署以及混合部署策略的优化模型；利用纽约市和多伦多的真实城市数据（地理参考公共AED目录、EMS报告的OHCA事件数据、网约车出行数据），对不同容量分配方案进行仿真与对比分析。

Result: 纽约市中，移动AED带来的平均响应时间改善超过2.5分钟，并在约55%-70%的AED容量为移动式时达到最优；超出该范围后，因固定覆盖减少导致优势与可靠性下降。多伦多市中，平均改善接近4分钟，并在接近全移动配置时趋于平稳。两城市呈现不同的最优混合模式。

Conclusion: 移动AED能够实质性改善急救可及性，但固定与移动AED的最佳组合必须根据当地的覆盖条件和出行环境量身定制，不存在普适的最优配置比例。

Abstract: Timely access to automated external defibrillators (AEDs) remains a major limitation of public-access defibrillation systems because stationary AED networks cannot adapt to spatial and temporal variation in out-of-hospital cardiac arrest (OHCA) demand. We study a platform-enabled mobile AED model in which AEDs are carried by existing urban ride-hailing fleets that can be dispatched to OHCA locations. Rather than assuming additional AED capacity, we hold the total AED supply fixed and compare alternative allocations between stationary and mobile deployment, including hybrid policies that retain both forms of coverage. Our analysis leverages real-world urban data from New York City and Toronto, integrating georeferenced public AED inventories, EMS-reported OHCA incident data, and ride-hailing mobility data. The results show that mobile AEDs can substantially improve both response times and reliability, but the benefits depend on how AED capacity is divided between stationary and mobile deployment. In New York City, average response-time gains exceed 2.5 minutes and peak when approximately 55 to 70 percent of AED capacity is mobile; beyond this range, both gains and reliability decline as stationary coverage is reduced. In Toronto, average gains approach 4 minutes and then plateau as the system moves toward a fully mobile configuration. These contrasting patterns show that mobile AEDs can materially improve access, but the best mix of stationary and mobile AEDs must be tailored to local coverage and mobility conditions.

</details>


### [114] [Dimensionless Controls of Plasticity Under Alternating Tasks: From Evolutionary Biology to Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23889)
*Owen Skriloff*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究深层神经网络在交替训练于两组布尔标签集时的可塑性，发现其受两个无量纲控制参数支配：任务分歧r和可达范围ηT。推导出可塑性下界，并通过9,720条轨迹的ANOVA分析验证r、η、T主导可塑性，而中性集大小影响可忽略。最优可达范围遵循ηT*∝r^-1.18的逆幂律，表明从生物环境中幸存下来的类比是动力学的而非几何的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可塑性在环境变化下的重要性横跨进化生物学和持续学习两个领域。然而，已有的基因型-表型图谱研究提出了多种生物学因素来控制可塑性，这些控制是否能在梯度下降这一深度学习翻译过程中保留下仍是开放问题。作者旨在建立一个最小深度学习类比系统，检验哪些生物学控制机制在翻译到梯度下降训练后仍然有效。

Method: 构建一个最小深度学习类比模型：网络交替训练于两组布尔标签集。将四种生物学因素重新解释为训练动力学量，将其简化为两个无量纲控制参数——任务分歧r（不一致标签的比例）和可达范围ηT（学习率η与切换周期T的乘积）。理论推导出两个可塑性下界：r单独决定乌托邦距离的极值几何下限，r与ηT联合限制遗忘。通过9,720条训练轨迹进行ANOVA分析验证各因素贡献，并拟合最优可达范围的幂律关系。

Result: ANOVA分析确认r、η、T是支配可塑性的主要因素，而生物背景中强调的中性集大小影响可忽略不计。最优可达范围遵循近似逆幂律ηT*∝r^-1.18，为仅凭任务分歧设定最优可达范围提供了启发式规则。该结果揭示了从生物学翻译到深度学习中幸存的是动力学类比而非几何类比。

Conclusion: 从生物学可塑性研究中幸存到深度学习梯度下降背景下的核心洞见是动力学层面而非几何层面。该最小系统将可塑性简化为两个无量纲控制参数，使得可塑性可以通过物理学和工程学中其他驱动系统的视角来理解，为跨领域统一可塑性理论提供了新途径。

Abstract: Plasticity under changing environments is central to both evolutionary biology and continual learning. Motivated by recent work on genotype--phenotype maps, we study a minimal deep-learning analogue where a network is trained alternately on two Boolean label sets, and ask which biological controls of plasticity survive the translation to gradient descent. Reinterpreting four proposed biological factors as quantities of training dynamics, we find the system reduces to two dimensionless controls: the task disagreement $r$, the fraction of disagreeing labels, and the reach $ηT$, the product of learning rate and switching period. We derive two bounds on plasticity: $r$ alone fixes an extremal geometric floor on the utopia distance, while $r$ and $ηT$ jointly bound forgetting. Across 9,720 trajectories, an ANOVA confirms that $r$, $η$, and $T$ dominate, while the effect of neutral-set size (emphasized in the biological setting) is negligible. The optimal reach itself follows an approximate inverse power law $ηT^{*}\propto r^{-1.18}$, yielding a heuristic that sets the optimal reach $ηT^*$ from the task disagreement alone. The analogy that survives is therefore dynamical rather than geometric, and our setting enables a view of plasticity through the lens of other driven systems in physics and engineering.

</details>


### [115] [The Shadow Price of Intelligence: Quality Degradation in LLM Inference as a Supply Chain Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23986)
*Elioth Sanabria*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文论证LLM服务提供商在算力受限时采取的降级策略（如路由到更小模型、削减推理、截断上下文）在传统成本核算下看似省钱，但实际是错误的：降级回答会以一定概率失败，失败要么转化为重试（在负载最高时人为扩大到达率），要么流失客户（摧毁终身价值）。作者用报童模型、几何重试乘数和两状态瞬态队列三类经典原语建模，证明静态下存在廉价模型在满意度上节能但单位满意度消耗更大容量的可测区域，折扣在容量紧张时反转；动态下反应式节流可能越过点火阈值制造更多流量，且释放规则低于降级均衡会把瞬态涌流变成永久降级状态。异质客户下节流是重试膨胀负载下的运输问题，最优策略按临界比率分类配给智能，对偶变量（智能的影子价格）可毫秒级计算边际查询定价。随机分析反而强化论点：点火边界获得预测宽度，噪声惩罚贴边运行的反应式策略。结论：拥塞下节流不是成本杠杆而是需求杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务提供商在算力受限时普遍以降级服务（小模型、削减推理、截断上下文）应对拥塞，行业成本核算认为这省钱。作者指出该核算错误，因为它以客户购买答案时的查询定价，而忽略降级回答的失败概率及其连锁后果（重试膨胀到达率、客户流失摧毁终身价值），故需重新建模推理分配问题。

Method: 使用三个经典原语建模：(1) 报童模型，缺货成本为流失的客户终身价值；(2) 几何重试乘数，回收产品是"不满"；(3) 两状态瞬态队列，到达率因重试而内生。综合静态容量分析、动态节流点火/释放规则分析、异质客户的运输问题建模与对偶影子价格分析，以及随机过程的边界宽度与噪声惩罚分析。

Result: 静态上存在非空可测区域，其中廉价模型在"单位满意答案"上节能却消耗更多容量，折扣恰在容量紧张时反转；动态上反应式节流可越过点火阈值制造更多流量，低释放规则会把瞬态涌流固化为永久降级；异质客户下最优节流策略按临界比率逐类配给智能，影子价格可按类按小时毫秒级计算；随机分析使点火边界获得预测宽度，并显示噪声惩罚贴边界运行的反应式策略。

Conclusion: 在拥塞条件下，节流不应被视为成本削减杠杆，而应被视为需求管理杠杆。行业通行的降级省钱成本会计是错的，因为它忽略重试膨胀与客户流失的终身价值损失；正确的推理分配需按容量约束下影子价格定价，并避免触发重试点火与永久降级陷阱。

Abstract: Large language model providers are compute constrained, and their universal response to congestion is to degrade service: route queries to smaller models, cut reasoning effort, truncate context. The industry's accounting says this saves money. We show the accounting is wrong, because it prices a query when the customer buys an answer. A degraded answer fails with some probability, and a failed answer either returns as a retry, inflating arrivals when the system is most loaded, or departs as churn, destroying lifetime value on a ledger no cost dashboard displays. We model inference allocation with three classical primitives: a newsvendor whose stockout cost is churned lifetime value, a geometric retry multiplier in which the recycled product is dissatisfaction, and a two-regime transient queue whose arrival rate is made endogenous by retries. Statically, there is a nonempty, measurable regime in which a cheaper model saves energy per satisfied answer while consuming strictly more capacity per satisfied answer, so the discount inverts exactly when capacity binds. Dynamically, a reactive throttle fired during a surge can cross an ignition threshold beyond which it manufactures more traffic than it sheds, and a release rule set below the degraded equilibrium converts a transient surge into a permanent degraded regime. With heterogeneous customers, throttling is a transportation problem in retry-inflated load whose optimal policy rations intelligence by critical ratio, class by class, and whose dual, the shadow price of intelligence, prices a marginal query by class and by hour; closed-form trajectories make it computable in milliseconds. Stochastic analysis sharpens rather than erodes the thesis: the ignition boundary acquires a predicted width, and noise punishes the reactive policy that parks the system against it. Under congestion, throttling is not a cost lever but a demand lever.

</details>


### [116] [Existence of Relaxed Equilibrium for Time-Inconsistent Mean Field Games: A Set-Valued Fixed Point Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24016)
*Zhenhua Wang,Zhou Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域连续时间时间不一致平均场博弈的松弛均衡存在性问题，通过构造含Young测度稳定拓扑的松弛反馈策略与Wasserstein连续人口流乘积空间上的均衡映射，利用Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg不动点定理证明了松弛均衡的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间不一致平均场博弈中，由于代理人偏好随时间不一致，标准NE/ME均衡概念难以直接处理，而松弛（relaxed）均衡提供了一种更灵活、更一般的处理框架。现有文献对有限时域连续时间情形下的松弛均衡存在性缺乏系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: (1) 在松弛反馈策略（赋予Young测度稳定拓扑）与人口流（限制在具有一致矩和有界时间正则性的Wasserstein连续流紧凸集）的乘积空间上工作；(2) 对每个策略-流对建立辅助值函数的均匀Sobolev与Hölder估计，并证明其在Young测度收敛与Wasserstein均匀收敛下的稳定性；(3) 通过对Fokker-Planck方程的对偶论证建立人口流映射的连续性（因Young测度收敛仅给出受控漂移的弱-*收敛）；(4) 构造具有非空紧凸值的集值最优响应/一致性映射并应用Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg不动点定理。

Result: 证明了所得不动点同时满足局内人博弈（intra-personal game）的均衡响应条件与平均场一致性条件，由此确立了松弛均衡的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文为有限时域连续时间时间不一致平均场博弈提供了一个一般性的松弛均衡存在性理论框架，其关键创新在于将Young测度拓扑与Fokker-Planck对偶论证相结合，克服了弱收敛带来的技术困难。

Abstract: This paper studies the existence of relaxed equilibria for finite-horizon continuous-time time-inconsistent mean field games. We work directly on the product space of relaxed feedback policies and population flows. The policy component is endowed with the stable topology of Young measures, while the population component is restricted to a compact convex set of Wasserstein-continuous flows with uniform moment and time-regularity bounds. For every policy-flow pair, we establish uniform Sobolev and Hölder estimates for the associated auxiliary value function and prove its stability under Young-measure convergence of policies and uniform Wasserstein convergence of population flows. We also establish continuity of the induced population-flow map. The latter requires a duality argument for the Fokker-Planck equations because Young-measure convergence yields only weak-$*$ convergence of the controlled drifts. We then construct a set-valued best-response/consistency map with nonempty compact convex values and apply the Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg fixed-point theorem. The resulting fixed point satisfies both the equilibrium response condition of the intra-personal game and the mean field consistency condition, , thereby establishing the existence of a relaxed equilibrium

</details>


### [117] [Safe Distributed Generalized Nash Equilibrium Seeking via Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24077)
*Yihan Meng,Weijian Li,Lacra Pavel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带耦合约束集的非合作博弈中的广义纳什均衡（GNE）求解问题，通过引入控制屏障函数（CBF）来设计GNE求解动力学，确保安全约束（即耦合约束集）的前向不变性，并在全信息和部分信息两种设置下均实现了GNE的渐近收敛，同时扩展到多积分器系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带耦合约束集的非合作博弈中，分布式GNE求解需要确保约束集（编码了安全规范）被满足，即保证安全性。现有方法在求解GNE时往往缺乏对约束集前向不变性的严格保证，因此需要将控制屏障函数引入GNE求解动力学设计中，以在求解过程中强制执行安全约束。

Method: 通过在GNE求解动力学中引入控制屏障函数（CBF），在动力学设计层面将耦合约束集编入约束条件，确保约束集的前向不变性。针对全信息（每个玩家知道所有其他玩家的决策）和部分信息（仅知道邻居玩家的决策）两种设置分别设计动力学，并进一步推广到多积分器系统。通过理论证明验证耦合约束集的前向不变性、动力学平衡点与精确GNE的一致性以及渐近稳定性。

Result: 理论分析表明：所设计的动力学能够保证耦合约束集的前向不变性（安全性），动力学平衡点与博弈的精确GNE一致，且系统渐近稳定。该结果在全信息和部分信息两种设置下均成立，且可推广到多积分器动力学系统。数值仿真验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于控制屏障函数的GNE求解动力学设计方法，能够在对分布式GNE求解过程中同时保证安全约束的满足，为带耦合约束集的非合作博弈提供了兼具安全性和收敛性的求解框架，并展示了其在不同信息结构和系统动力学下的可扩展性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE) seeking in non-cooperative games with coupled constraint sets. Specifically, we aim to enforce safety for distributed GNE seeking, whereby the safety specifications are encoded in the coupled constraint set. To achieve this, we introduce the control barrier function (CBF) in the design of the GNE seeking dynamics. We design the dynamics for both full- and partial-information setting, where each player has knowledge of the decision information of all other players or only neighboring players, respectively. We justify the proposed dynamics by showing that the coupled constraint set is forward invariant, the equilibrium of the dynamics coincides with the exact GNE of the game, and the dynamics is asymptotically stable. Furthermore, we extend the approach to games where the agents are multi-integrators. Numerical simulations are provided to verify our results.

</details>


### [118] [Quadratic Optimization over Probability Measures with Coupling Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24184)
*Hoang Anh Tran,Yong Sheng Soh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对二次目标函数、决策变量为概率测度的优化问题的凸松弛层级框架，扩展了标准的广义矩问题（GMP）建模范围，并与矩-SOS层级理论相结合，在边际约束条件下证明了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义矩问题框架要求目标函数是线性的，而许多实际应用（如Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输问题和能量景观最小化）中目标函数关于决策变量是二次的，超出了该框架的适用范围，需要新的建模与求解方法。

Method: 提出了基于基础空间乘积空间上概率测度搜索的凸松弛层级；引入了一种超越传统矩阵的（半）正定概念；在边际约束假设下建立层级收敛到全局最优解的理论保证；在多项式目标函数的额外假设下，提出有限维半定规划的矩-SOS型层级。

Result: 在决策变量满足Kantorovich型边际约束的条件下，证明了所提层级收敛到全局最优解；在多项式目标的情况下，提出的有限维SDP层级的最优解收敛到原始二次测度优化的最优解，并通过数值实验进行了验证。

Conclusion: 本文为二次（以及更一般多项式型）测度优化问题提供了将SOS思想从传统多项式优化扩展到非线性测度问题的路线图，拓展了该框架的适用范围。

Abstract: We consider solving an optimization instance in which the objective is quadratic and where the decision variable is a probability measure. Our class of problems are motivated by applications arising from optimal transport (with the Gromov-Wasserstein problem being a prominent example) as well as energy landscape minimization. Because the objective depends quadratically on the decision variable, our class of problems fall outside the standard modeling framework of the Generalized Moment Problems (which requires the objective to be linear). To this end, we propose a hierarchy of convex relaxations based on searching over probability measures over products of the base space. These have a natural interpretation with the moment Sum-of-squares hierarchy-a prominent framework for solving polynomial optimization instances, which we adapt to accommodate probability measures. A key conceptual contribution is to introduce a notion of positive-semidefiniteness that extends the usual notion over matrices. Under the assumption that the decision variables satisfy certain marginal constraints (as in the Kantorovich formulation of the optimal transport problem), we establish convergence of our hierarchy towards the globally optimal solution. Under the additional assumption that the objective is a polynomial, we propose a moment-SOS type hierarchy of finite dimensional semidefinite programs whose optimal solution converges to that of the original quadratic optimization over measures. We demonstrate our framework with numerical experiments. More generally, optimization over measures where the objective and/or constraint depends on the decision in a polynomial way is a fundamental problem. It is hoped that our work provides a road-map as to how the ideas of the SOS-ordinarily developed for polynomial optimization-may be applied to a broader class of non-linear problems involving measures.

</details>


### [119] [A Line-Search-Free Coordinate Proximal Predictor-Corrector Method for Monotone Absolute Value Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24346)
*Haotian Wang,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究残差单调性下的绝对值方程（AVE）Ax-|x|=b，通过系数矩阵的对称部分刻画该性质从而允许非对称矩阵，提出一种基于AVE特定前向-近端分解的坐标近端预测-校正（CPPC）方法。该方法在最大近端残差坐标上进行精确标量近端更新生成预测点，并利用正对齐恒等式实现无需回溯的全残差分离超平面校正；在缓存当前矩阵积的前提下，每次迭代仅需一次新的完整矩阵-向量乘积。在非空解集条件下证明了Fejér单调性、全序列收敛性及O(K^{-1/2})的最优迭代残差界；正单调性余量进一步保证对任意右端项的唯一可解性和全局线性收敛。数值实验表明，降低单次迭代工作量在某些情形下可获得更短的求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对值方程（AVE）Ax-|x|=b在互补问题、优化及科学计算等领域有广泛背景。现有方法多要求系数矩阵具备对称性或其他强结构限制，且每迭代工作量较高。作者旨在：(1) 通过系数矩阵的对称部分刻画残差单调性，突破对称限制，允许非对称矩阵；(2) 设计一种每迭代仅需一次矩阵-向量乘积的高效算法，以降低单步计算成本，提升求解效率。

Method: 提出坐标近端预测-校正（CPPC）方法。其核心步骤包括：(1) 基于AVE特有的前向-近端分解，在最大近端残差坐标上执行精确标量近端更新，生成预测点；(2) 利用正对齐恒等式对预测点的全残差进行分离超平面校正，该校正过程无需回溯（即无需线搜索或步长调整），提高了迭代效率；(3) 通过缓存当前矩阵积，每次迭代仅需计算一次新的完整矩阵-向量乘积。理论分析上，利用正单调性余量建立唯一可解性与线性收敛性。

Result: 在非空解集条件下证明了方法的Fejér单调性、全序列收敛性以及O(K^{-1/2})的最优迭代残差收敛界；当存在正单调性余量时，进一步证明了对于任意右端项b系统均有唯一解，且方法全局线性收敛到唯一解。数值实验表明，在特定问题情形下，降低的单次迭代计算量能够转化为更短的总体求解时间。

Conclusion: 本文通过对称部分刻画残差单调性，将AVE求解拓展至非对称矩阵情形；提出的CPPC方法兼具理论收敛保证（Fejér单调性、全序列收敛、次线性残差界及线性收敛特例）和高效的计算特性（每迭代一次矩阵-向量乘积、无回溯校正），在单步成本与收敛速度之间取得了良好的平衡，为大规模绝对值方程的求解提供了新的高效工具。

Abstract: We consider the absolute value equation (AVE) $Ax-|x|=b$ under residual monotonicity. We characterize this property through the symmetric part of the coefficient matrix, thereby allowing nonsymmetry, and propose a coordinate proximal predictor-corrector (CPPC) method based on an AVE-specific forward-proximal decomposition. An exact scalar proximal update on the largest proximal-residual coordinate generates a predictor point, while a positive-alignment identity certifies the ensuing full-residual separating-hyperplane correction without backtracking. With the current matrix product cached, each iteration requires one new full matrix-vector product. For a nonempty solution set, we prove Fejér monotonicity, whole-sequence convergence, and an $O(K^{-1/2})$ best-iterate residual bound. A positive monotonicity margin further ensures unique solvability for every right-hand side and global linear convergence. Numerical results identify regimes in which the reduced per-iteration work yields shorter solution times.

</details>


### [120] [Coupling-Aware Sales and Operations Planning with Forced Co-Production in Polymer Production Plants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24399)
*Mansur Arief,Yan Pratama Akhra,Bilal Ahmadi,Pradipta Apsarini,Iwan Vanany*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"产品簇增强毛利"(AGPPC)的框架，将传统的单产品吨位排名指标转化为按耦合小时排名的指标，并附带最优性证明，以解决联产工厂（尤其是聚合物工厂）中并行产线共享单一散装原料时的生产排产问题。在印尼某聚合物生产商的实际数据上，该耦合感知指标单独可将运营利润提升7.6%，完整优化可提升28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单产品毛利率等盈利指标假设各生产线可独立调度，但在耦合工厂（如聚合物工厂中并行产线同时从单一散装原料取料）中，这一前提不成立，会导致价值破坏的生产计划。因此需要一种能感知耦合关系并保持实用简便性的排产指标。

Method: 提出AGPPC框架：(1) 将单产品每吨排名转化为每耦合小时排名；(2) 结合联产列概念与规划问题的流体松弛建立理论；(3) 构建集成双线性混合整数规划模型；(4) 提供McCormick线性化的基准模型以给出有证书的紧致界。

Result: 在印尼某聚合物生产商的真实运营数据上，仅使用耦合感知的AGPPC指标就能将运营利润较现行实践提高7.6%，而完整优化模型可提高28%。

Conclusion: 耦合感知的盈利指标在保持简化实用性的同时，能够克服传统单产品毛利率在联产工厂中的误导性问题，为销售与运营计划(S&OP)提供可认证最优性的排产决策支持。

Abstract: Profitability metrics, such as the single-product gross profit margin, constitute the standard toolbox to prioritize products in sales and operations planning (S&OP). These metrics leverage the premise that production lines can be committed independently, which endows them with a simple per-ton ranking that a planner can compute and use easily in practice. However, coupled plants, especially polymer plants whose parallel lines draw simultaneously on a single bulk feed, can fundamentally undermine this premise and mislead to value-destroying production plans. We propose a framework built on what we call the Augmented Gross Profit for Product Clusters (AGPPC), which converts the per-ton ranking metric into a per-coupled-hour ranking that carries a per-instance optimality certificate. We present the theory of AGPPC, which combines the co-production column concept with a fluid relaxation of the planning problem, alongside an integrated bilinear mixed integer program of the plant and a McCormick-linearized baseline that provides certified bounds for it, and we demonstrate its effectiveness on real operational data from an Indonesian polymer producer, where the coupling-aware metric alone raises operating profit by 7.6% and the full optimization by 28% over current practice.

</details>


### [121] [Mean field game model for human capital accumulation under budget constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24417)
*Saeed Sadeghi Arjmand*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个确定性一阶均值场博弈模型，用于人类资本积累问题。在该模型中，连续统的个体投资于教育和培训，以最小时间达到某一资格水平。学习效率同时取决于个体当前人力资本水平和人口分布（刻画知识溢出与社会互动），学习所需资金来源于现有人力资本产生的收入，由此形成状态约束（金融资源须保持非负）。对固定的人口流量，个体问题被建模为带时间依赖动力学的状态约束最小时间最优控制问题；通过动态规划原理和Hamilton-Jacobi方程分析值函数，并在适当正则性和凸性假设下刻画最优学习努力。最优反馈诱导出人口分布演化的连续性方程，均值场博弈最终被表述为关于人口流量的不动点问题。通过构造紧不变集并运用Schauder不动点定理，证明了在每个有限人口时域上至少存在一个均衡，而个体最小时间问题仍在无限时间时域上表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于：人类资本积累是一个重要的社会经济问题，个体在有限资源（时间和金钱）约束下追求最快达到更高资格水平。同时，个体学习生产力会受到社会互动和知识溢出（即整个人口分布）的影响，这种相互依赖关系需要采用均值场博弈框架来建模。此外，学习资金来源于现有资本产生的收入，这使得模型必须引入状态约束（金融资源非负），增加了问题的复杂性。作者旨在提供一个严格的数学框架，保证这一复杂博弈均衡的存在性。

Method: 本文方法包括：1) 将个体问题建模为状态约束最小时间最优控制问题，具有时间依赖动力学；2) 利用动态规划原理和Hamilton-Jacobi方程分析值函数；3) 在正则性和凸性假设下刻画最优学习努力（最优反馈策略）；4) 通过最优反馈构造人口分布演化的连续性方程；5) 将均值场博弈表述为人口流量的不动点问题；6) 构建紧不变集并应用Schauder不动点定理证明均衡存在性。值得注意的是，个体问题在无限时间时域上表述，而均衡存在性仅在有限人口时域上证明。

Result: 本文的主要结果包括：证明了状态约束最小时间最优控制问题中最优控制和轨道的存在性；通过动态规划原理和Hamilton-Jacobi方程对值函数进行了分析；在适当假设下刻画了最优学习努力的特征；通过构造紧不变集并运用Schauder不动点定理，证明了在任意有限人口时域上至少存在一个均值场博弈均衡。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：该人类资本积累的均值场博弈模型在有限人口时域上存在至少一个均衡。个体层面的最小时间问题在无限时间时域上成立，而群体层面的均衡在有限时域上获得，这种时间尺度的分离反映了个体长期积累与群体短期交互的层次结构。这一结果为理解知识溢出和社会互动下的人力资本形成提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We study a deterministic first-order mean field game model for human capital accumulation in which a continuum of agents invest in education and training to reach a certain qualification level in minimum time. The productivity of learning depends on both the individual's current level of human capital and the distribution of the population, modeling knowledge spillovers and social interactions. Learning is financed by the income generated by existing human capital, leading to a state constraint requiring the financial resources to remain nonnegative throughout the evolution. For a fixed population flow, we formulate the individual problem as a state-constrained minimum-time optimal control problem with time-dependent dynamics. We establish the existence of optimal controls and trajectories, analyze the associated value function through the Dynamic Programming Principle and a Hamilton--Jacobi equation, and characterize the optimal learning effort under suitable regularity and convexity assumptions. The optimal feedback induces a continuity equation describing the evolution of the population distribution. The resulting mean field game is formulated as a fixed-point problem for the population flow. By constructing a compact invariant set and applying Schauder's fixed-point theorem, we prove the existence of at least one equilibrium on every finite population horizon, while the individual minimum-time problem remains formulated on the infinite time horizon.

</details>


### [122] [Modelling Renewable Curtailment and Constraints in Ireland's Electricity System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24464)
*Richard Nayer,Sam Hodges,Waqquas Bukhsh,Claver Chitambo,Chanura Wijeratne*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文描述了爱尔兰电力系统的电力市场及运行流程，并将其转化为混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，用于估算可再生能源发电的削减（Curtailment）与约束（Constraint）。完整的数学模型在简单算例和爱尔兰实际输电网络模型上进行了测试，结果验证了模型的有效性，同时指出了模型的局限性与未来改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源在爱尔兰电力系统中的占比不断提高，准确估算可再生能源的削减与约束量对于电力系统规划、市场设计及政策制定至关重要。然而，现有工具往往难以精确捕捉电力市场运行与物理输电阻塞之间的交互效应，因此需要一种能够同时反映市场规则和输电网络约束的建模方法。

Method: 本文首先对爱尔兰电力系统的市场机制和运行流程进行系统性梳理，然后将其关键特征转化为一个混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型。该模型对电力市场调度与输电网络的物理约束进行了联合建模，以区分削减（受整体供需平衡或市场因素影响导致的发电减少）与约束（受输电网络容量限制导致的发电减少）。模型在简单示例和爱尔兰实际输电网络上进行了验证测试。

Result: 测试结果表明，所提出的MILP模型能够按要求准确捕捉削减与约束效应，在简单算例和爱尔兰实际输电网络模型上均运行正常。同时，测试也揭示了模型当前存在的一些局限性。

Conclusion: 该MILP模型为爱尔兰电力系统中可再生能源削减与约束的量化分析提供了有效工具，能够支持更准确的系统规划与决策；但模型仍需在计算效率和精确度方面进一步改进，以便更好地反映实际电力系统的复杂性。

Abstract: This paper describes the electricity markets and operational processes in the Irish power system and translates them into a Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model. The model is designed to estimate renewable generation Curtailment and Constraint. A full mathematical formulation is presented and tested on both a simple example and a realistic model of the Irish transmission network. The results show that the proposed formulation works as intended, capturing curtailment and constraint effects, while also highlighting model limitations and directions for future improvement.

</details>


### [123] [A constraint dissolving inexact penalty method for optimization problems with geometric constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24542)
*Xiaoxi Jia,Leander Lerch,Stefan Streif,Manuel Schaller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对非凸几何约束的约束溶解映射框架，并基于此开发了约束溶解不精确惩罚方法来求解具有一般集合成员约束和非凸几何约束的优化问题。该方法在温和的渐近莫尔杜霍维奇正则性条件下收敛，且每个可行聚点均为莫尔杜霍维奇稳定点。数值实验表明其在经典等式、互补、稀疏和低秩约束问题上与带保护的增广拉格朗日方法竞争力相当，并显著优于惩罚分解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何约束优化问题在机器学习、金融和控制等领域有广泛的应用，而约束溶解方法是解决这类几何约束的强大算法工具。然而，现有约束溶解方法文献中依赖于较强的约束规范性条件，本文旨在通过更温和的条件（渐近莫尔杜霍维奇正则性）来放松这些要求，从而扩大方法的适用范围。

Method: 本文首先提出了一个适用于非凸几何约束的约束溶解映射框架，该框架能够将几何约束转化为更易处理的形式。在此基础上，开发了一种约束溶解不精确惩罚方法，用于求解具有一般集合成员约束和可能非凸几何约束的优化问题。算法通过迭代求解一系列不精确惩罚子问题来逼近原问题的最优解。

Result: 理论方面，建立了所提算法的收敛性分析，证明每个可行聚点都是莫尔杜霍维奇稳定点，且所需条件仅为温和的渐近莫尔杜霍维奇正则性，这显著弱于现有文献中约束溶解方法所需的约束规范性条件。数值方面，在经典等式、互补、稀疏和低秩约束优化问题上的实验表明，该方法在解质量上与带保护的增广拉格朗日方法相当，且显著优于惩罚分解方法。

Conclusion: 本文所提出的约束溶解不精确惩罚方法为非凸几何约束优化问题提供了一种理论保证完善且数值表现优异的求解框架。通过放宽收敛性所需的约束规范性假设，该方法比现有约束溶解方法更具普适性，同时数值实验验证了其在实际问题中的竞争力和高效性。

Abstract: Optimization problems with geometric constraints have a broad range of applications, including machine learning, finance, and control. A powerful algorithmic tool to resolve these geometric constraints are constraint dissolving methods. To this end, we propose a framework for constraint dissolving mappings for nonconvex geometric constraints. Leveraging these, we develop a constraint dissolving inexact penalty method to solve optimization problems with general set-membership constraints and possibly nonconvex geometric constraints. We establish the convergence of the proposed algorithm and prove that every feasible accumulation point is Mordukhovich stationary. Notably, we rely only on mild asymptotic Mordukhovich regularity, which is significantly weaker than the constraint qualifications adopted in the existing literature on constraint dissolving methods. Numerical experiments addressing classical equality-, complementarity-, sparsity-, and low-rank constrained optimization problems demonstrate that the proposed method is competitive with the safeguarded augmented Lagrangian method in terms of solution quality and significantly outperforms the penalty decomposition method.

</details>


### [124] [Optimal Allocation of Embedding Dimensions under Finite-Sample Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24592)
*Vasileios E. Papageorgiou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将类别型预测变量的嵌入维度选择建模为约束分配问题，证明了嵌入能力可以根据显式的近似-估计权衡在不同类别预测变量间分配，并推导出闭式分配规则（每个预测变量的维度与其近似值相对参数成本的平方根成正比），实验和真实医疗数据应用验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别型预测变量的嵌入维度通常通过启发式调参选择，虽然它直接影响模型复杂度、近似质量和有限样本泛化能力，但缺乏一个原则性的理论框架来指导维度分配。

Method: 将嵌入维度选择形式化为约束分配问题：通过潜在类别表示的奇异值尾部刻画近似误差，估计误差随总嵌入复杂度增加而增加；在固定全局嵌入预算和可处理的近似模型下，推导出闭式分配规则——分配给每个预测变量的维度与其近似值相对参数成本的平方根成正比。

Result: 仿真实验支持所提出的近似-估计权衡解释，并表明该分配规则相比标准均匀分配和基于基数的启发式方法提高了预算效率，尤其在预算约束紧张和预测变量异质性显著时。真实医疗数据应用进一步展示了预测准确性和概率校准方面的改进。

Conclusion: 本文将嵌入维度分配确立为一个有原则的有限样本优化问题，而非纯启发式的建模选择，为嵌入维度选择提供了理论依据和可操作的分配规则。

Abstract: The embedding dimension of categorical predictors is usually selected through heuristic tuning, although it directly affects model complexity, approximation quality, and finite-sample generalization. This paper formulates embedding dimension selection as a constrained allocation problem. The main contribution is to show that embedding capacity can be allocated across heterogeneous categorical predictors according to an explicit approximation-estimation tradeoff. We characterize approximation error through the singular-value tail of the latent category representation, while estimation error increases with total embedding complexity. Under a fixed global embedding budget and a tractable approximation model, this leads to a closed-form allocation rule in which the dimension assigned to each predictor is proportional to the square root of its approximation value relative to its parameter cost. Simulation experiments support the proposed approximation-estimation interpretation and show that the allocation rule improves budget efficiency relative to standard uniform and cardinality-based heuristics, particularly when the budget is binding and predictor heterogeneity is substantial. A real-data healthcare application further shows improvements in predictive accuracy and probabilistic calibration. Overall, the results establish embedding-dimension allocation as a principled finite-sample optimization problem rather than a purely heuristic modeling choice.

</details>


### [125] [Bounded Linear Programs for Data-Driven Optimal Control via Moment-Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24709)
*Andrea Martinelli,Lucia Pezzetti,Niklas Schmid,Florian Dorfler,John Lygeros*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续空间中无限时域、无模型非线性最优控制问题，探讨了线性规划（LP）方法的实际应用瓶颈——有界解难以一致获得的问题，并利用矩匹配技术推导出基于数据集和成本向量的充分有界性条件，同时讨论了非线性系统和多项式特征的实际设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性规划（LP）公式为连续空间中的无限时域、无模型非线性最优控制提供了一种概念上优雅的方法，但其实际应用受到两个主要限制：维度灾难（curse of dimensionality）以及难以一致地获得有界解（bounded solutions）。后者是本文重点解决的痛点，因为它严重限制了LP方法在实践中的可靠性。

Method: 本文采用矩匹配（moment-matching）技术，从可用数据集和LP的成本向量出发，推导出保证LP获得有界解的充分条件。此外，针对非线性系统和多项式特征，提出了相应的实际设计方法。

Result: 成功推导出基于数据集和成本向量的充分有界性条件，并给出了非线性系统中多项式特征的实际设计指导。

Conclusion: 通过矩匹配技术为LP公式的有界解问题提供了理论保证和实用设计准则，克服了LP方法在实际应用中的关键障碍之一，使其在非线性最优控制中的可行性得到提升。

Abstract: Linear programming (LP) formulations offer a conceptually elegant approach to infinite-horizon, model-free nonlinear optimal control in continuous spaces. However, in addition to the curse of dimensionality, their practical use is limited by the difficulty of consistently obtaining bounded solutions. In this work, we use moment-matching techniques to derive sufficient boundedness conditions in terms of the available dataset and the cost vector of the LP. Moreover, we discuss practical design methods for nonlinear systems and polynomial features.

</details>


### [126] [A Novel Fractional-Order Accelerated Gradient Descent Method for Nonlinear Optimization with Application to Posture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24766)
*Barsha Shawa,Md Abu Talhamainuddin Ansarya,Soundararajan Ganesanb,Minvydas Ragulskisb*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Caputo分数阶导数的加速梯度下降方法（CFAGD），适用于光滑和一类非光滑无约束优化问题。该方法结合自适应β参数和分数阶导数的记忆特性，通过Armijo条件选择步长，在强凸和有界Hessian假设下证明了线性收敛，并通过数值实验（包括神经网络示例）验证了其比现有方法更快、更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的整数阶梯度下降方法在优化过程中缺乏对历史迭代信息的利用，收敛速度和稳定性有待提升。分数阶导数具有非局部性和记忆特性，能够更好地刻画迭代过程中的历史影响，从而有望改善优化算法的性能。

Method: 提出CFAGD方法，核心包括：（1）引入自适应β^k参数，在迭代过程中启发式更新以改善搜索方向；（2）采用Caputo分数阶导数结合自适应β^k参数，保留非整数阶导数的记忆特性；（3）基于Armijo条件的不精确线搜索选择合适步长；（4）通过一个正参数缩放步长，改善迭代点接近最优值时的行为，生成下降序列。

Result: 在强凸性和有界Hessian假设下，证明了所提方法的线性收敛性；数值实验（包括基于神经网络的示例）表明CFAGD方法在收敛速度和稳定性方面均优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: CFAGD方法通过将分数阶微积分与自适应参数策略相结合，有效利用了历史信息并提升了梯度下降算法的性能，为无约束优化提供了一种新的高效求解框架。

Abstract: This article proposes a Caputo fractional accelerated gradient descent (CFAGD) method for unconstrained optimization problems that is applicable to both smooth and a class of non-smooth objective functions. The proposed approach incorporates an adaptive (\b{eta}k)-parameter, which is heuristically updated throughout the iterative process to improve the search direction. Furthermore, the method employs the Caputo fractional derivative together with the adaptive (\b{eta}^k)-parameter, thereby preserving the memory characteristics associated with non-integer-order derivatives. A suitable step-size is selected via an inexact line-search technique based on the Armijo condition. The central idea is to scale the step-size by a positive parameter to improve the behavior of the iterates as they approach an optimal point, thereby generating a descent sequence. Under strong convexity and bounded Hessian assumptions, linear convergence of the proposed method is established. Numerical validations, including neural-network-based examples, further indicate that the CFAGD method can achieve faster and more stable performance than competing approaches.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [A Comment on Local Hypercube Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23667)
*Keyou Zhuo,Tian-Shun Chen,Kilar Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了高维分拆电荷函数构造中出现的局部不等式的维度无关解析证明，替代了先前工作中低维穷举计算和高维数值测试的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作中，高维分拆电荷函数构造中涉及的局部不等式仅在低维中通过穷举计算验证，并在选定的更高维度中进行数值测试，缺乏通用的解析证明，因此需要维度无关的严格证明来完善理论。

Method: 采用解析方法，分别对奇数维和偶数维构造中的局部不等式给出维度无关的证明，不再依赖穷举计算或数值验证。

Result: 成功给出了这些局部不等式的维度无关解析证明，覆盖了奇维和偶维两种构造情形。

Conclusion: 本文填补了高维分拆电荷函数理论中不等式证明的空白，用解析证明取代了先前的计算验证，使结果在所有维度上得到严格保证。

Abstract: This comment gives dimension-independent analytic proofs of the local inequalities arising in the odd- and even-dimensional constructions of higher-dimensional partition charge functions. The prior works established these inequalities by exhaustive computation in low dimensions and tested them numerically in selected higher dimensions.

</details>


### [128] [Hartree--Fock coercivity for twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23884)
*Kevin D. Stubbs,Maciej Zworski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了手性扭转双层石墨烯平坦带中Hartree-Fock泛函的极小化问题，证明了在半填充条件下存在恰好两个极小化子，并首次证明了这两个极小化子处Hessian算子的严格强制性（coercivity），该性质源于组分带的Chern数之差，可被视为"谱隙"的软版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扭转双层石墨烯的平坦带模型中理解强关联电子系统的基态结构，特别是Hartree-Fock泛函的极小化子的性质，以及它们与拓扑不变量（Chern数）之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用连续版本的模型（基于Tarnopolsky-Kruchkov-Vishwanath的手性模型），通过分析Hartree-Fock泛函在极小化子处的Hessian算子，利用组分带Chern数之差来证明Hessian的严格强制性。

Result: 在半填充条件下恰好存在两个极小化子（分别占据两个子带之一），并且在这两个极小化子处Hartree-Fock泛函的Hessian是严格强制的，这一性质源自Chern数之差。

Conclusion: Hartree-Fock极小化子的Hessian严格强制性可以作为"谱隙"的软版本，揭示了拓扑不变量（Chern数）与基态稳定性之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We study the minimisers of the Hartree--Fock functional for flat bands in the chiral model of twisted bilayer graphene introduced by Tarnopolsky, Kruchkov, and Vishwanath. Our model is a continuous version of the discrete model considered by Becker, Lin and Stubbs in previous works and the first result is a direct analogue of main theorems of those papers: at half filling of the flat band of multiplicity two, there are exactly two minimisers, obtained by occupying either one of the two sub-bands. (A more general version of this result, stressing its topological origins, was presented in Appendix A.8 of the Lecture Notes by Tao-Zworski.) The new contribution is the strict coercivity of the Hessian of the Hartree--Fock functional at the two minimisers. It is a consequence of the difference between the Chern numbers of the component bands and can be optimistically considered as a soft version of a ``spectral gap''.

</details>


### [129] [On the Dirichlet-to-Neumann conformal invariant of bounded planar domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23899)
*Alexey Kokotov,Dmitrii Korikov,Marina Nenasheva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种新的共形类规范代表——带测地边界的平坦锥面（LC图），并在此坐标下推导出Dirichlet边界问题行列式的变分公式。通过Schottky对偶和Burghelea-Friedlander-Kappeler公式，显式计算了多连通域上的Dirichlet-to-Neumann共形不变量detΛ/|Γ|，其表达式仅依赖于域的Schottky对偶的周期。该公式是Guillarmou和Guillopé通过Ruelle与Selberg zeta函数所得结果的初等对应物，并与Wentworth的渐近结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为任意连通度的有界域上的Dirichlet-to-Neumann（DN）共形不变量找到一种更初等、更显式的计算公式，以替代或补充之前Guillarmou和Guillopé通过Ruelle与Selberg zeta函数给出的较为复杂的表述。

Method: 借助Mandelstam-Giddings-Wolpert图的类比，构造LC图作为共形类的规范代表；在该图的自然坐标下推导Dirichlet边界问题行列式的变分公式；取LC图的Schottky对偶，结合Burghelea-Friedlander-Kappeler公式与全纯微分模空间上Laplacian行列式的已知变分公式，最终显式计算DN不变量detΛ/|Γ|。

Result: 得到了DN共形不变量的显式公式，该公式仅依赖于Schottky对偶的周期。该公式与Wentworth关于所有边界分量收缩时DN不变量渐近行为的最新结果一致，并据此确定了公式中的积分常数。

Conclusion: 所得到的公式是Guillarmou和Guillopé基于zeta函数表达式的初等化精简版本，且与Wentworth的渐近结果相吻合，为DN共形不变量的计算提供了一种更为直接、只用周期数据的途径。

Abstract: Using an analogue of Mandelstam-Giddings-Wolpert diagrams, we introduce a new canonical representative of the conformal class of a bounded domain of arbitrary connectivity in $\mathbb{C}$ as a flat conical surface with geodesic boundary (a "truncated light-cone diagram", simply LC-diagram in the sequel). The space of these diagrams can be provided with natural coordinates. We derive variational formulas for the determinant of the Dirichlet boundary problem on a LC-diagram with respect to these coordinates. Then passing to the Schottky double of the LC-diagram, making use of the Burghelea-Friedlander-Kappeler formula and the known variational formulas for determinants of Laplacians on the moduli space of holomorphic differentials lead to an explicit computation of the Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DN) conformal invariant $\frac{{\rm det}Λ}{|Γ|}$ (here $Λ$ is the DN operator on the boundary, $Γ$, of a multi-connected domain and $|Γ|$ is the length of the boundary). The resulting formula (which uses the periods of the Schottky double of the domain only) presents a conspicuously elementary counterpart to the formulas of Guillarmou and Guillopé who had expressed the DN invariant through the Ruelle and Selberg zeta-functions. Our formula agrees with the recent result of Wentworth on the asymptotics of the DN invariant as all the boundary components except one shrink, we have used this result to fix the undetermined constant of integration in our formula.

</details>


### [130] [Non-equilibrium modelling of polyatomic gases: generic framework and 11-moment model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24100)
*Masrakain Ahmad,Anil Kumar,Anirudh Singh Rana*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于GENERIC框架的热力学一致模型，用于描述多原子气体的非平衡行为，通过11矩描述统一了理性不可逆热力学和扩展不可逆热力学，并在氮气激波结构中验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多原子气体的非平衡热力学建模存在挑战，RIT和EIT两个框架各有局限且缺乏统一结构。需要一个热力学一致、满足第二定律、并能提供物理意义清晰的闭合关系的统一框架。

Method: 将GENERIC（广义非平衡可逆-不可逆耦合方程）与11矩描述相结合，显式构造Poisson矩阵和摩擦矩阵，纳入平移温度、内部温度以及温度张量，从热力学原理自然推导所有闭合关系。

Result: 模型保证熵产生非负，所有闭合关系自然涌现。在氮气激波结构计算中，与DSMC模拟和实验数据在中等马赫数范围内吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该GENERIC-11矩框架成功统一了RIT和EIT，为多原子气体的非平衡热力学提供了一个热力学一致、物理意义明确且可验证的建模平台。

Abstract: In this study, we present a thermodynamically consistent framework for modelling polyatomic gases by integrating the \textit{general equation for non-equilibrium reversible-irreversible coupling} (GENERIC) with an 11-moment description. Our formulation unifies \textit{rational irreversible thermodynamics} (RIT) and \textit{extended irreversible thermodynamics} (EIT) into a single GENERIC-compatible structure that guarantees compliance with the second law of thermodynamics through the explicit construction of Poisson and friction matrices. By including translational and internal temperatures, as well as a temperature tensor, the model offers a well-structured and physically meaningful representation of non-equilibrium behavior in polyatomic gases. The entropy production is inherently non-negative, and all closure relations emerge naturally from thermodynamic principles. As a test of applicability, we compute the shock wave structure in nitrogen gas, validating the framework against Direct Simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC) simulations and experiments up to moderate Mach numbers.

</details>


### [131] [Boundary Free Energies, Quenched Mixing, and Gibbs-State Selection in Disordered Ising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24261)
*Mauris Chueng,Hexiang Wang,Keheng Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最近邻无序Ising模型中的边界自由能和半空间响应。证明了固定深度切向压力的存在性及其导数与极限边界响应的几乎处处一致性；在淬火指数边界响应混合条件下，证明了Gibbs态选择独立性和有限深度压力的指数收敛；证明了均匀单自旋混合导致DLR唯一性和高斯法向局域化，并在特定条件下验证了所需的混合条件；最后在受限Dobrushin区域建立了全表面极限，并给出了限制一般表面和刚度结论的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序系统（如随机Ising模型）中的边界自由能和表面响应行为是统计力学中核心且尚未完全理解的问题。特别是边界响应的存在性、Gibbs态的选择唯一性以及表面自由能极限的建立，在一般无序条件下缺乏系统性的数学证明。

Method: 采用严谨的数学分析方法，包括：(1)证明固定深度切向压力的存在性并利用导数与响应的一致性；(2)引入淬火指数边界响应混合条件，证明Gibbs态选择独立性和有限深度压力指数收敛；(3)利用均匀单自旋混合条件推导DLR唯一性和高斯法向局域化；(4)在(2d−1)E tanh(β|J|)<1条件下验证混合条件的充分性；(5)在受限Dobrushin区域建立全表面极限并构造反例以界定一般结论的适用范围。

Result: 主要结果包括：边界切向压力的存在性及几乎处处导数一致性；在指数边界响应混合条件下Gibbs态选择独立性与指数收敛速率；均匀单自旋混合蕴含DLR唯一性和高斯法向局域化；建立了混合条件的可验证充分条件(2d−1)E tanh(β|J|)<1；在受限Dobrushin区域建立全表面极限，并通过反例表明一般情况下的表面自由能和刚度结论不成立。

Conclusion: 本文为无序Ising模型的边界自由能和半空间响应提供了严格的数学框架，建立了若干关键的存在性和唯一性定理，同时通过反例清晰地划定了结果的适用范围，说明在一般无序条件下表面自由能极限和刚度性质需要更精细的假设。

Abstract: We study boundary free energies and half-space responses in nearest-neighbor disordered Ising models. First, we prove the existence of fixed-depth tangential pressure and identify its derivative almost everywhere with the limiting boundary response. Second, under quenched exponential boundary-response mixing, we prove Gibbs-state selection independence and exponential convergence of finite-depth pressures. We further show that uniform one-spin mixing yields DLR uniqueness and Gaussian normal localization, and verify the required mixing conditions whenever $(2d-1)\mathbb E\tanh(β\vert{}J\vert{})<1$. Finally, we establish an all-face surface limit in the bounded Dobrushin regime and provide counterexamples that delimit general surface and stiffness claims.

</details>


### [132] [Joint moments of characteristic polynomials in the circular Jacobi ensemble and Painlevé equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24423)
*Thomas Bothner,Fei Wei*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了Circular Jacobi系综中特征多项式及其导数的联合矩与非线性Painlevé方程之间的联系。有限N情形下，这些联合矩由σ-Painlevé V方程的解刻划（适用于所有允许范围内的实数矩指数）；在大N缩放极限下，联合矩可由σ-Painlevé III'方程的解表示。作为应用，作者回答了Assiotis等人提出的一个公开问题，并将涉及高阶导数的联合矩结果从实数参数推广到复数参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机矩阵理论中特征多项式的矩结构与Painlevé方程之间的深层联系，解决Assiotis等人遗留的公开问题，并将已有结果从实数参数推广到复数参数，扩展理论适用范围。

Method: 利用Circular Jacobi系综的结构性质，结合σ-Painlevé V和σ-Painlevé III'方程的可积性理论，通过有限N精确刻画与大N缩放极限分析建立联合矩的积分/微分方程表示。对涉及高阶导数的情形，采用类似的技术路线并进行参数域的推广。

Result: 1) 证明有限N下所有允许范围内实数矩指数的联合矩由σ-Painlevé V方程刻划；2) 在大N极限下某些矩指数范围内联合矩由σ-Painlevé III'方程表示；3) 回答了Assiotis等的公开问题（该随机变量的特征函数与复数参数的σ-Painlevé III'方程相关）；4) 将高阶导数情形的联合矩结果从实参数推广至复参数。

Conclusion: 本文系统地揭示了Circular Jacobi系综中特征多项式联合矩与Painlevé可积系统之间的内在联系，不仅解决了公开问题，还将相关结果推广到更一般的复数参数情形，为随机矩阵理论与可积系统交叉领域提供了新的重要进展。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a connection between joint moments of characteristic polynomials and their derivatives in the Circular Jacobi Ensemble, a generalisation of the Circular Unitary Ensemble, and solutions of nonlinear Painlevé equations. For finite $N$, we show that these joint moments are characterised by a solution of the $σ$-Painlevé V equation for all real moment exponents in their admissible range. Under an appropriate large-$N$ scaling limit, we further prove that the limiting joint moments admit a representation in terms of a solution of the $σ$-Painlevé III$'$ equation for a certain range of moment exponents. As applications, we answer a question posed by Assiotis et al. in [Math. Physics. Anal. Geom. 25 (2022), no.2, Paper No. 15, 24pp, Remark 1.7] by showing that the characteristic function of a distinguished random variable is connected with the $σ$-Painlevé III$'$ equation for complex parameters. Furthermore, we investigate joint moments involving higher-order derivatives of characteristic polynomials in the Circular Jacobi Ensemble. As a consequence, we extend a result of Assiotis et al. in [Comm. Pure Appl. Math. 79 (2026), no. 7, 1771-1827, Theorem 1.11] concerning the joint moments of a sequence of random variables arising from the ergodic decomposition of Hua-Pickrell measures, from real parameters to complex parameters.

</details>


### [133] [Bound State Analysis of Rank-One Delta Interactions Supported by Deformed Hyperspheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24508)
*Hilal Demirdöğen,Fatih Erman,Merve Nur Sayar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究n维超球面上由小法向形变支持的秩一delta相互作用。结果表明，在O(ε)阶上，束缚态能量仅依赖于形变的平均法向位移<h>，等价于一个有效半径R-ε<h>的圆超球面；均值为零的形变在一阶不改变束缚态能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超球形壳问题中几何形变对量子束缚态的影响，特别是秩一delta相互作用在n维空间中的谱性质对支撑几何微小变化的敏感程度。

Method: 利用旋转不变扇区，将未形变超球面的束缚态方程通过修正贝塞尔乘积I_{(n-1)/2}(νR)K_{(n-1)/2}(νR)刻画。对法向形变X_ε(ω)=(R-εh(ω))ω进行一阶微扰分析，考察束缚态能量对形变参数的依赖。

Result: 在一阶微扰下，束缚态能量仅取决于平均法向位移⟨h⟩，形变超球面在O(ε²)精度内谱等价于有效半径R-ε⟨h⟩的圆超球面。均值为零的形变在一阶不改变束缚态能量。

Conclusion: 对秩一delta相互作用，超球面几何形变对束缚态能量的一阶效应仅由平均位移决定，这是谱蕴含的对称性——一阶谱不变需要形变保持面积平均意义上的对称性。

Abstract: We study rank-one delta interactions supported by small normal deformations of an \(n\)-dimensional hypersphere \(S_R^n\subset\mathbb{R}^{n+1}\). The interaction is defined by the normalized surface measure on the support and therefore corresponds to the rotationally invariant sector of the hyperspherical shell problem. For the undeformed hypersphere, we derive the bound-state equation for \(E=-ν^2\) in terms of the modified Bessel product \[
  I_{\frac{n-1}{2}}(νR)K_{\frac{n-1}{2}}(νR). \] We then consider an inward normal deformation \[
  X_\varepsilon(ω)=(R-\varepsilon h(ω))ω,
  \qquad ω\in S^n,
  \qquad 0<\varepsilon\ll1. \] Our main result is that, to first order in \(\varepsilon\), the bound-state energy depends only on the average normal displacement \[
  \langle h\rangle
  =
  \frac1{|S^n|}\int_{S^n}h(ω)\,dΩ_n(ω). \] Equivalently, the deformed hypersphere is spectrally equivalent, up to \(O(\varepsilon^2)\), to a round hypersphere with effective radius \(R-\varepsilon\langle h\rangle\). In particular, mean-zero deformations do not change the rank-one bound-state energy at first order.

</details>

### [134] [Autoformalizing the calculation of $π_3(S^2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23721)
*Daniel Carranza,Chunyi Liu,Emily Riehl,Egbert Rijke*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文报告了使用Codex自动形式化同伦类型论中π₃(S²)计算的结果，该形式化基于agda-unimath库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI驱动的自动形式化在复杂数学证明中的能力，特别是针对同伦类型论中π₃(S²)这样具有挑战性的计算。

Method: 使用Codex（AI代码生成模型）对homotopy type theory中π₃(S²)的计算进行自动形式化，利用agda-unimath库作为形式化基础。

Result: 报告了自动形式化实验的发现和结果，展示了Codex在复杂数学形式化任务上的表现。

Conclusion: 通过对π₃(S²)自动形式化的实验，验证了AI在辅助形式化高难度数学证明方面的可行性及其局限性。

Abstract: We report our findings and the results of our experiment of autoformalizing the homotopy type theoretic computation of $π_3(S^2)$ in agda-unimath using Codex.

</details>


### [135] [Transport based embeddings with topological guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23762)
*Erik Carlsson,John Carlsson*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最优输运理论的数据凝聚方法，通过将点云数据编码为随机矩阵并定义基于KL散度的势函数，证明该势函数是c-凸的，从而保证凝聚过程在拓扑层面上保持输入数据的同伦等价性。该方法在COIL图像数据集和SYMSOL基准上成功恢复潜在流形结构，优于标准PCA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云数据（如图像集合、MCMC样本或随机游走状态）常具有简单但被噪声、高维环境和欧氏距离失效所掩盖的内在几何结构。UMAP和t-SNE等现有方法虽能凝聚数据，但依赖启发式选择，且无法保证输出反映输入数据的拓扑结构。因此需要一个具有拓扑保持保证的凝聚方法。

Method: 将数据编码为正m×n随机矩阵Q，在概率单纯形Δ_n上定义势函数ψ(p)=log Σ_i exp(-KL(p,q_{i•}))。作者证明ψ在成本函数c(p,q)=KL(p,q)意义下是c-凸的（最优输运理论意义）。通过关联的输运映射压缩噪声方向，同时保持拓扑：ψ的超水平集与c-共轭函数的超水平集同伦等价，后者构成一个凝聚的、可重采样的拓扑空间族，可视为alpha形状的连续类比。

Result: 在COIL图像数据集上成功恢复相机角度的圆环结构（而标准PCA管道产生虚假同调），在SYMSOL姿态估计基准中从45,000个四面体视图恢复了商空间SO(3)/A_4。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维带噪点云的拓扑保持凝聚提供了理论保证，克服了UMAP/t-SNE等方法的启发式局限，能够准确恢复潜藏的低维流形拓扑结构。

Abstract: Point clouds arising in image collections, samples from Markov chain Monte Carlo, or states of a random walk, often have a simple underlying geometry which is obscured by noise, high ambient dimension, and the failure of Euclidean distance to reflect similarity. Methods such as UMAP and t-SNE condense such data into usable form, but rely on heuristic choices and provide no guarantee that the output reflects the topology of the input. We introduce a condensation method that comes with such a guarantee. Encoding the data as a positive $m\times n$ stochastic matrix $Q=(q_{ij})$, for instance the transition matrix of a random walk on the point cloud, we define a potential function $ψ(p)=\log \sum_{i} \exp(-KL(p,q_{i\bullet}))$ on the probability simplex $Δ_n$, where $KL(p,q)$ is the Kullback-Leibler divergence, and prove that $ψ$ is $c$-convex in the sense of Optimal Transport Theory for the cost function $c(p,q)=KL(p,q)$. The associated transport map collapses noisy directions while provably preserving topology: the super-level sets of $ψ$ are homotopy equivalent to those of a $c$-conjugate function, whose image is a condensed, resampleable family of topological spaces which can be interpreted as a continuous analog of an alpha shape. We demonstrate the method by recovering the circle of camera angles from the COIL image dataset, where a standard PCA pipeline produces spurious homology, and the quotient $SO(3)/A_4$ from $45{,}000$ views of a tetrahedron in the SYMSOL pose-estimation benchmark.

</details>


### [136] [The Stable Adams Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23797)
*Eric M. Friedlander*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为原始Adams猜想的谱版本提供了多种证明变体。方法利用F-空间（Segal Gamma-空间）建立与J-映射和Adams运算相关的谱映射之间的不稳定和稳定同伦等价。技术核心是将刚性Artin-Mazur平展同伦理论应用于定义在正特征域Witt向量上的单纯概形交换图，并发展了X-纤维化及l-完备X-纤维化的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adams猜想是代数拓扑与代数几何交叉领域的重要结果，涉及J-映射与Adams运算之间的关系。作者旨在提供该猜想在谱（spectra）层面的多种证明变体，并通过F-空间框架统一处理不稳定与稳定的同伦等价，同时建立一套系统的X-纤维化理论以支撑证明。

Method: 采用F-空间（Segal Gamma-空间）作为核心工具，利用刚性Artin-Mazur平展同伦理论处理定义在正特征域Witt向量上的单纯概形交换图。论文专门发展了X-纤维化和l-完备X-纤维化的完整理论框架，并以此为基础证明谱映射间的不稳定与稳定同伦等价关系。

Result: 成功建立了多种版本的Adams猜想谱证明，确立了与J-映射和Adams运算相关的谱映射之间的不稳定和稳定同伦等价，并完备发展了X-纤维化与l-完备X-纤维化的理论体系。

Conclusion: 通过F-空间的统一框架和刚性平展同伦理论，为Adams猜想的谱版本提供了多种自足且严格的证明变体，同时贡献了一套适用于该领域的X-纤维化理论工具。

Abstract: We provide proofs of several variants for spectra of the original Adams Conjecture. Our approach uses F-spaces (also known as Segal Gamma-spaces), resulting in establishing both unstable and stable homotopy equivalences between maps of spectra associated to the J-map and the Adams operations. We employ a rigid version of Artin-Mazur etale homotopy theory applied to commutative diagrams of simplicial schemes defined over the Witt vectors of fields of positive characteristic. Much of this text is devoting to developing a theory of X-fibrations and l-complete X-fibrations over the F-spaces we consider.

</details>


### [137] [Multiplicatively cohomological Tambara functors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23973)
*David Chan*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类特殊的Tambara函子，称为乘法上同调Tambara函子。首先证明了Balchin、Quigley和Spitz的猜想：每个乘法上同调Tambara函子也是加法上同调的。在此基础上，证明了乘法上同调Tambara函子的中山引理，并作为应用，刻画了局部Tambara函子上有限生成投射模自由的条件，该结果特别适用于常数C_{p^n}-Tambara函子Z_p。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于深化对Tambara函子这一代数对象的理解，特别是解决Balchin、Quigley和Spitz提出的关于乘法上同调Tambara函子与加法上同调Tambara函子之间关系的猜想，并通过建立相应的中山引理来推广交换代数中的经典结果，为局部Tambara函子上模结构的研究提供新工具。

Method: 本文的主要方法包括：首先构造或利用乘法上同调Tambara函子的结构性质来证明两个上同调概念之间的蕴含关系；接着在已建立的基础上，将经典交换代数中的中山引理推广到乘法上同调Tambara函子的框架下；最后通过中山引理来分析有限生成投射模的自由性条件，并将其应用于常数C_{p^n}-Tambara函子。

Result: 主要结果有三：(1) 证明了Balchin、Quigley和Spitz的猜想，即乘法上同调Tambara函子必然是加法上同调的；(2) 证明了乘法上同调Tambara函子的中山引理；(3) 给出了在局部Tambara函子上有限生成投射模为自由模的充分条件，并验证了该条件对常数C_{p^n}-Tambara函子Z_p成立。

Conclusion: 本文通过证明猜想和建立中山引理，揭示了乘法上同调Tambara函子的深层结构性质，并借此解决了局部Tambara函子上模的自由性问题，特别是证明了常数C_{p^n}-Tambara函子Z_p上的有限生成投射模在一定条件下是自由的，为未来Tambara函子理论及其在等变同伦论中的应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: We study a nice class of Tambara functors, called multiplicatively cohomological Tambara functors. The first result is a proof of a conjecture of Balchin, Quigley, and Spitz showing that every multiplicatively cohomological Tambara functor is also additively cohomological. Building on this, we prove Nakayama's lemma for multiplicatively cohomological Tambara functors. As an application, we identify a condition under which finitely generated projective modules over local Tambara functors are always free. This applies, in particular, to the constant $C_{p^n}$-Tambara functor $\underline{\mathbb{Z}}_p$.

</details>


### [138] [On a Weak Form of the Topological Hedetniemi Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24487)
*Hamid Reza Daneshpajouh*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于既非素数幂阶循环群也非广义四元数群的群G，广义拓扑Hedetniemi猜想的失败程度可以任意大：对任意n≥1，都存在两个有限自由G-单纯复形，每个的拓扑指标为n，但其乘积的拓扑指标为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bui和Daneshpajouh已证明当群G既非素数幂阶循环群也非广义四元数群时，广义拓扑Hedetniemi猜想失败，并构造了拓扑指标为1的复形对。本文旨在展示这种失败并非仅限于指标1的情况，而是可以推广到任意拓扑指标，从而使失败在最强意义上成立。

Method: 本文通过构造性方法，对任意整数n≥1，显式构造两个有限自由G-单纯复形，使每个复形的拓扑指标均为n，但它们的乘积的拓扑指标为0。该构造推广了Bui和Daneshpajouh的原始构造，将拓扑指标从1提升到任意正整数n。

Result: 对任意整数n≥1，在G既非素数幂阶循环群也非广义四元数群的条件下，存在两个有限自由G-单纯复形，各自拓扑指标为n，但其乘积的拓扑指标为零。这意味着广义拓扑Hedetniemi猜想的弱形式对这些群在最强可能意义上失败。

Conclusion: 本文表明拓扑Hedetniemi猜想在广义G-空间情形下的失败程度是任意大的，而非仅仅是边缘性的反例。这彻底排除了通过限制指标大小来挽救该猜想弱形式的可能性。

Abstract: The topological Hedetniemi conjecture asserts that the topological index of the product of two \(\mathbb{Z}/2\)-complexes is equal to the minimum of their respective indices. Bui and Daneshpajouh studied a natural generalization of this conjecture to \(G\)-spaces and proved that this generalized conjecture fails whenever the group \(G\) is neither cyclic of prime-power order nor generalized quaternion. More precisely, for any such group \(G\), they constructed finite free \(G\)-simplicial complexes, each having topological index one, whose product has topological index zero.
  The purpose of this note is to demonstrate that this discrepancy can be made arbitrarily large. Indeed, for every integer \(n\geq 1\), we show that there exist two finite free \(G\)-simplicial complexes, each of topological index $n$, whose product has topological index zero, provided that \(G\) is neither cyclic of prime-power order nor generalized quaternion. In particular, the corresponding weak form of the generalized topological Hedetniemi conjecture fails in the strongest possible sense for those groups.

</details>

### [139] [Large scale theoretical investigation of the phase diagram of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$ at fractional fillings: agreements and contradictions with current experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23675)
*Heqiu Li,Jiabin Yu,Xiaodong Xu,B. Andrei Bernevig,N. Regnault*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对扭转双层MoTe₂在实验相关转角（2.13°–4°）和空穴填充下进行了全面的精确对角化研究，比较了单能带/双能带每谷投影以及第一谐波连续模型与无参数DFT模型，系统揭示了分数陈绝缘体（FCI）、复合费米液体（CFL）和Pfaffian态在不同填充与转角下的存在性及多能带物理的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转双层MoTe₂是探索相互作用驱动的拓扑和关联物态的重要平台，但已有的理论研究多依赖简化的单能带投影和第一谐波连续模型，无法定量解释实验中观察到的FCI层级、CFL以及缺失态等现象。作者旨在建立一个统一的数值基准，厘清多能带物理在定量预测中的关键作用。

Method: 采用连续模型莫尔能带，分别进行每谷单能带（1BPV）和每谷双能带（2BPV）精确对角化计算，后者包含相互作用驱动的能带混合效应；同时对比两种连续模型：广泛使用的第一谐波模型和参数自由的DFT"免拟合"模型。扫描多个实验相关转角和空穴填充分数。

Result: 2BPV计算在奇分母填充下重现了实验观察到的FCI层级（约3.7°附近），包括ν=-2/3、-3/5、-4/7的稳健不可压缩态，正确预测ν=-3/7处无FCI，并在ν=-1/3处倾向于电荷密度波而非FCI；在ν=-1/2处，1BPV显示清晰的CFL特征而2BPV中的能带混合使CFL失稳；在θ≈2.13°的ν=-3/2处未发现非阿贝尔Pfaffian态的证据。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了扭转双层MoTe₂中关联和拓扑相的统一数值基准，并明确指出多能带物理对于与实验进行定量比较是不可或缺的：能带混合效应不仅改变FCI的稳定性层级，还会破坏CFL和Pfaffian态，需要在实际建模中予以充分考虑。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive exact-diagonalization study of interaction-driven phases in twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$ across experimentally relevant twist angles ($2.13^\circ$--$4^\circ$) and hole fillings. Using continuum-model moiré bands, we compare the one-band-per-valley (1BPV) projection with a two-band-per-valley (2BPV) calculation that includes interaction-driven band mixing, and we benchmark both the widely used first-harmonic continuum model and a parameter-free DFT ``fitting-free'' model. At odd-denominator fillings, the 2BPV calculation reproduces the experimentally observed hierarchy of fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) around $θ\approx 3.7^\circ$, including robust incompressible states at $ν=-2/3$, $-3/5$, and $-4/7$ while correctly finding the absence of an FCI at $ν=-3/7$, and it favors a charge density wave ground state at $ν=-1/3$ over the FCI. At half filling $ν=-1/2$, the 1BPV calculation exhibits clear composite Fermi liquid (CFL) signatures, whereas the band mixing in 2BPV calculations destabilizes the CFL ground state. Finally, motivated by the Landau-level analogy at $θ\approx 2.13^\circ$, we test the proposed non-abelian Pfaffian state at $ν=-3/2$ in the fully-polarized spin sector but find no evidence for this state within the models and parameters studied. Our results establish a unified numerical benchmark for correlated and topological phases in twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$ and clarify where multi-band physics is essential for a quantitative comparison with experiments.

</details>


### [140] [Raman Circular Dichroism Reveals Higher-Order Quantum Geometry of Magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23683)
*Yong Chan Kim,Youngsu Choi,Kwang-Yong Choi,Kyusung Hwang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种规范不变的高阶磁振子量子几何理论，用于探测双磁振子拉曼圆二色性（RCD）。拉曼算子可分解为Berry联络、协变导数及Berry联络的乘积，产生了超越量子度量和Berry曲率的量子几何张量。将该理论应用于场极化Kitaev磁体，证明高阶几何张量控制了二色性响应，确立了RCD作为广义磁振子量子几何的光谱探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁振子量子几何主要局限于量子度量和Berry曲率等二阶几何量，缺乏对更高阶量子几何张量的系统研究，也缺少相应的光谱探测手段。

Method: 从规范不变原理出发，将双磁振子拉曼算子分解为Berry联络、协变导数和Berry联络乘积的组合，由此构建高阶量子几何张量；并将该理论框架应用于场极化Kitaev磁体进行具体验证。

Result: 证明了高阶几何张量（超越量子度量和Berry曲率）决定了双磁振子拉曼圆二色性响应；在Kitaev磁体中得到了具体验证。

Conclusion: 建立了磁振子高阶量子几何的规范不变理论框架，确立了拉曼圆二色性作为一种光谱探针，可探测广义磁振子量子几何。

Abstract: We develop a gauge-invariant theory of higher-order magnon quantum geometry probed by two-magnon Raman circular dichroism (RCD). The Raman operator decomposes into Berry connections, covariant derivatives, and products of Berry connections, giving rise to quantum geometric tensors beyond the quantum metric and Berry curvature. Applying this theory to a field-polarized Kitaev magnet, we show that higher-order geometric tensors govern the dichroic response. Our results establish RCD as a spectroscopic probe of generalized magnon quantum geometry.

</details>


### [141] [Reality and Complexity of $F$-symbols in $2+1$d Topological Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23693)
*Matthew Buican,Peter Huston,Jiannis K. Pachos*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了任意子理论中F符号为何在许多情况下可取实数这一现象背后的物理机制：通过适当的辫子电荷共轭对称性，复共轭的F符号与原F符号之间仅差一个规范变换；当该对称性满足"平坦"条件（即扭结Frobenius-Schur数据尊重分级）时，可将局部变换平凡化从而获得实规范。该框架统一了众多此前看似无关的例子（包括若干Chern-Simons理论），并首次构造出具有固有复F符号的幺正辫带范畴，展示了实的阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子理论的F符号编码了融合的结合性，是该理论最基本也最微妙的数据之一，其微妙性主要来自规范依赖性。尽管许多辫子任意子理论中存在使所有F符号为实的规范，但此前缺乏统一的组织原则来解释这一现象。作者旨在揭示这一"实性"现象背后的物理机制，并提供一个系统性的统一框架。

Method: 作者从幺正辫带范畴出发，假设其具有合适的辫子电荷共轭对称性，证明复共轭后的F符号可通过规范变换与原F符号关联。进而将寻找实规范的问题归结为这些变换所满足的条件。当电荷共轭对称性"平坦"（即相关的扭结Frobenius-Schur数据或推广的Kawanaka-Matsuyama数据尊重某种分级）时，局部变换可被平凡化，从而确保实规范存在。此框架还用于构造具有实F符号阻碍的幺正辫带范畴新例。

Result: 该框架统一了大量此前分散的例子，包括一些F符号难以直接计算的Chern-Simons理论族。此外，作者构造了一个幺正辫带范畴，实现了对实规范存在性的新阻碍，证明其F符号本质上必须是复数。

Conclusion: 作者通过辫子电荷共轭对称性与扭结Frobenius-Schur数据的分级结构，为任意子理论F符号的实性提供了统一的物理机制解释，同时发现了实的规范存在的新的阻碍来源，深化了对F符号规范自由度本质的理解。

Abstract: The $F$-symbols of an anyon theory encode the associativity of fusion and constitute some of the theory's most fundamental and, simultaneously, subtle data. Much of the subtlety lies in the gauge-dependence of the $F$-symbols. Despite their generic complexity, many braided anyon theories admit gauges in which all $F$-symbols are real, a phenomenon for which no general organising principle has been known. We identify a physical mechanism underlying this reality. For a unitary ribbon fusion category admitting an appropriate braided charge-conjugation symmetry, we show that the complex-conjugated $F$-symbols are related to the original ones by a gauge transformation. Finding a real gauge is thereby reduced to a condition on these transformations. When the charge-conjugation symmetry is suitably "flat,'' or equivalently when the associated "twisted'' Frobenius-Schur (or "generalized'' Kawanaka-Matsuyama) data respects a grading, these local transformations can be trivialised and a real gauge exists. This framework unifies a broad range of previously disparate examples, including families of Chern-Simons theories whose $F$-symbols are difficult to directly compute. Finally, we exhibit a unitary ribbon category that realizes a novel obstruction to the existence of a real gauge and therefore has inherently complex $F$-symbols.

</details>


### [142] [Quantum-geometry stabilization of dilute fractional Chern insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24013)
*Ying-Xing Ding,Li-Min Zhang,Wen-Tong Li,D. L. Zhou,Wu-Ming Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种中心装饰的kagome模型，通过调控中心位点hopping参数$t_2$来优化量子几何（Berry曲率和Fubini-Study度量），从而抑制电荷有序竞争态，增强低填充分数Chern绝缘体的多体稳定性，在$\nu=1/3$和更脆弱的$\nu=1/5$填充下均实现了Laughlin型分数Chern绝缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低填充分数Chern绝缘体不稳定，因为电荷有序相会与分数拓扑液体强烈竞争。传统上仅优化能带平坦度不足以稳定这些拓扑相，需要寻找额外的调控手段。

Method: 提出中心装饰的kagome模型（受几何可调人工晶格启发），以中心位点hopping $t_2$作为量子几何的直接调控旋钮。通过精确对角化研究，系统分析trace-condition偏差、电荷序竞争、多体稳定性、低能谱、谱流、准空穴与纠缠计数、静态结构因子以及总多体Chern数$C_{\mathrm{tot}}=1$。

Result: 将$t_2$调离平坦度最优的kagome极限可减小trace-condition偏差、抑制电荷序、增强$\nu=1/3$和$\nu=1/5$填充下的多体稳定性。$\nu=1/5$的稳定窗口在邻近相互作用剖面（包括第三近邻排斥和弱最近邻混合变化）下保持稳健。多种观测量一致支持Laughlin型分数Chern绝缘体。

Conclusion: 量子几何工程是超越单纯能带平坦度优化之外稳定稀薄分数Chern绝缘体的有效途径，为该领域提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Fractional Chern insulators have attracted broad interest as lattice analogs of fractional quantum Hall states without Landau levels. However, low-filling fractional Chern insulators are fragile because charge-ordered phases can compete strongly with the fractional topological liquid.
  Here, we propose a center-decorated kagome model, motivated by geometry-tunable artificial lattices, in which the center-site hopping $t_2$ provides a direct knob for the quantum geometry of an isolated $C=1$ flat band.
  Here quantum geometry refers to the Berry curvature and Fubini--Study metric, which determine the form factors of interactions projected into the Chern band. Exact diagonalization shows that tuning $t_2$ away from the flatness-optimized kagome limit reduces the trace-condition deviation, suppresses competing charge order, and enhances the many-body stability at both $ν=1/3$ and the more fragile $ν=1/5$ filling.
  At $ν=1/5$, this stability-enhanced window persists under nearby interaction profiles, including variations of the dominant third-neighbor repulsion and weak nearest-neighbor admixtures.
  Low-energy spectra, spectral flow, quasihole and entanglement counting, static structure factors, and the quantized total many-body Chern number $C_{\mathrm{tot}}=1$ consistently support Laughlin-like fractional Chern insulators.
  These results identify quantum-geometry engineering as a route to stabilizing dilute fractional Chern insulators beyond band-flatness optimization alone.

</details>


### [143] [Neutron scattering evidence for two-dimensionally coupled spin-dimerized antiferromagnetic lattice in α-Cu2P2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24141)
*B. Ghanta,K. S. Chikara,M. Ghanathe,L. Keller,D. Voneshen,A. K. Bera*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过非弹性中子散射、中子衍射、磁化测量和自旋波建模，全面解析了低维量子磁体α-Cu2P2O7的磁性基态和激发谱。结果表明它是一个bc面内二维耦合的自旋二聚化反铁磁晶格，主导intradimer交换J2=7.73 meV发生在第七近邻Cu-Cu对而非最近邻结构二聚体对，弱层间耦合（J5=0.03 meV）在TN=25 K下稳定长程反铁磁序，单离子各向异性打开能隙并驱动场致变磁转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-Cu2P2O7的微观磁模型一直存在争议，此前GGA+U理论预测与实验不符，需要综合实验手段结合精确自旋波建模来明确其磁性基态和交换耦合网络。

Method: 结合温度依赖的非弹性中子散射、中子衍射、磁化率/磁化测量以及全面的自旋波（spin-wave）建模，同时引入LDA-DFT理论计算进行交叉验证，系统分析交换耦合常数、各向异性项、能隙来源及磁场响应。

Result: （1）明确α-Cu2P2O7为bc面内二维耦合的自旋二聚化反铁磁体；（2）主导intradimer交换J2=7.73 meV发生在第七近邻Cu-Cu对（5.125 Å）而非最近的3.014 Å对；（3）确定J1、J3、J4为较弱的interdimer交换；（4）弱层间耦合J5=0.03 meV在TN=25 K驱动长程序；（5）识别出由畸变CuO5多面体产生的弱单离子各向异性，在自旋激发谱中打开能隙；（6）发现此前被忽略的约13 kOe处的变磁转变，建立H-T相图；（7）计算结果与LDA-DFT一致，与GGA+U预测相反。

Conclusion: 该研究最终解决了α-Cu2P2O7磁模型的争议，确认其为二维自旋二聚化反铁磁体，主导交换耦合并不遵循结构二聚体直觉，而是源于几何距离更远的Cu对。J5与各向异性项D共同产生两个不同反铁磁区中心的能隙，并驱动场致变磁转变。该工作为理解低维量子磁体中结构-磁交换关联提供了关键范例。

Abstract: The microscopic magnetic model of the low-dimensional quantum magnet alpha-Cu2P2O7 has remained controversial. We present a comprehensive study of its magnetic ground state and excitation spectrum using temperature-dependent inelastic neutron scattering, neutron diffraction, magnetization measurements, and comprehensive spin-wave modeling. Our results unambiguously establish alpha-Cu2P2O7 as a two-dimensionally coupled spin-dimerized antiferromagnetic (AF) lattice within the bc plane, with a dominant AF exchange J2 = 7.73 meV (hereafter referred to as "intradimer exchange") and weaker exchange couplings J1, J3, and J4 in the two-dimensional lattice (hereafter referred to as "interdimer exchange"), in agreement with LDA-based density functional theory and in contrast to previous GGA+U predictions. The dominant intradimer AF exchange is found between seventh-nearest-neighbor Cu-Cu ion pairs [d(Cu-Cu) = 5.125(3) A] rather than nearest-neighbor Cu-Cu ion pairs [d(Cu-Cu) = 3.014(1) A] of the structural dimers. Weak interlayer coupling (J5 = 0.03 meV) stabilizes long-range antiferromagnetic order below TN = 25 K. We further identify a weak single-ion anisotropy, associated with the distorted CuO5 polyhedra, that opens a gap in the spin-excitation spectrum and drives a field-induced metamagnetic transition. Systematic spin-wave calculations elucidate the distinct roles of interlayer coupling J5 and anisotropy term D in producing two distinct energy gaps at different antiferromagnetic zone centers. Complementary neutron diffraction and magnetization measurements as a function of applied magnetic field uncover a previously overlooked metamagnetic transition near 13 kOe and allow construction of the magnetic phase diagram in the H-T plane.

</details>


### [144] [Frustration-induced degenerate spin state with up-up-down-down ordering in corner-connected Heisenberg square-plaquettes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24172)
*Pritam Manna,A. K. Bera*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过Luttinger-Tisza最小化和大规模Monte Carlo模拟，系统研究了角连接方形plaquette海森堡模型的阻挫磁性，在(J1/|J2|, J3/|J2|)相图中发现了三种低温相：反铁磁相、铁磁相以及一种具有up-up-down-down (uudd)序的简并自旋态，并进一步揭示了该简并态在磁场下的负相关与正相关层状区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解阻挫量子磁体中几何挫败与自旋排列之间的复杂相互作用，特别是角连接方形plaquette结构中由多种交换相互作用（边缘、plaquette间及对角线）共同导致的磁有序行为，以填补该模型在经典极限下完整相图研究的空白。

Method: 结合两种互补方法：(1) Luttinger-Tisza (LT) 最小化傅里叶相互作用矩阵来获得零温候选有序波矢；(2) 大规模Monte Carlo (MC) 模拟在硬自旋约束下解析低温有限温度的有序倾向。通过LT线状最小值与MC模拟的对比，揭示简并DS态的多q特性。

Result: 在归一化平面(J1/|J2|, J3/|J2|)上获得经典低温磁相图，识别出三类低温柔性相：反铁磁(AF)、铁磁(FM)和简并uudd自旋态(DS)。DS态中LT显示沿qx=±qy的线状简并最小值，MC则发现准二维有序的硬自旋违背态。该态为多q结构，由对称相关模式组装，表现为对角线强反铁磁关联、角链接强铁磁关联、边沿关联被强烈抑制。磁场-温度相图揭示了正/负相关层区域被一条零层间相关曲线分隔。

Conclusion: 该模型在DS区域中展现出LT分析与MC模拟之间的深刻分歧——LT预测的高度简并线状最小值违背硬自旋约束，而MC揭示了一种由多q模式组合而成的准二维uudd自旋纹理，说明在强简并阻挫体系中，纯粹波矢分析不足以确定真实自旋序，必须结合约束条件下的数值模拟。磁场进一步调谐了层间的正负相关，暗示此模型具有丰富的调控潜力。

Abstract: We investigate frustrated magnetism of a corner-connected square plaquette Heisenberg model with exchange interactions along the edges $(J_1)$, along inter-plaquette links $(J_2)$, and along square diagonals $(J_3)$. Using Luttinger--Tisza (LT) minimization of the Fourier interaction matrix $\mathcal J(\mathbf q)$ together with large-scale Monte Carlo (MC) simulations, we obtain a classical low-temperature magnetic phase diagram in the normalized plane $(J_1/|J_2|, J_3/|J_2|)$. The two methods play complementary roles: the LT analysis provides the zero-temperature candidate ordering wave vectors, while the MC simulations elucidate the resulting ordering tendencies under the hard-spin constraint at low but finite temperatures. Three regimes emerge at low temperatures, an antiferromagnetic phase (AF), a ferromagnetic phase (FM) and a degenerate spin state with \textit{up-up-down-down (uudd)} ordering (DS). For the frustrated degenerate spinstate, LT exhibits line-like minima along $q_x=\pm q_y$ in the $hk$-plane, revealing a highly degenerate spin configuration which violates the hard-spin constraint. The MC results uncover a state with quasi-two-dimensional ordering. The DS regime is intrinsically multi-$\mathbf{q}$: the ordered texture assembles itself from symmetry-related modes on the lines, producing a ``\textit{uudd}'' spin arrangement comprising of distinctive strong antiferromagnetic correlations on diagonals, ferromagnetic correlations on corner links, and highly suppressed correlations on the edges. The field-temperature phase diagram for a representative parameter point in the DS regime, determined by MC simulations, yields field induced distinct regions of negatively and positively correlated layers. These two regions are separated by a curve corresponding to negligible inter-layer correlations.

</details>


### [145] [Critical bifurcation and deconfined quantum criticality in an interacting cluster Ising chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24248)
*Sourabh,Bachana Beradze,Mikheil Tsitsishvili,Alexander Nersesyan,Titas Chanda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过张量网络方法和弱耦合场论研究了带最近邻相互作用的cluster伊辛链，揭示了从SU(2)_2临界点到Ising与Gaussian临界线分叉、再到deconfined量子临界线及浮游相的一系列丰富量子相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子多体系统中对称性破缺如何影响临界行为，特别是研究O(3)对称性被约化为C_3后，原始c=3/2临界点如何分叉为不同的临界线，以及Landau不相容相变（deconfined量子临界）的实现机制。

Method: 采用张量网络方法（数值模拟）结合弱耦合场论（解析分析）。未加相互作用时通过Jordan-Wigner变换将模型分解为具有O(3)味对称性的三份全同Majorana链；加入相互作用后利用弱耦合场论捕获对称性从O(3)到C_3的约化及相应临界行为。

Result: 发现相互作用使O(3)味对称性约化为C_3循环子群，三份Majorana链分裂为单态和双重态，其能隙分别闭合导致Ising(c=1/2)和Gaussian(c=1)两条临界线的分叉。排斥/吸引相互作用在临界线之间分别打开第二铁磁相和无序相。Gaussian临界线实现具有涌现O(2)对称性的deconfined量子临界线，其中临界指数连续变化但满足八顶点弱普适类的参数无关关系β=(2ν-1)/4。更强的排斥作用使该线张开为具有非公度代数关联的浮游相，通过BKT相变进入。弱耦合理论之外，数值模拟揭示通过一级相变到达的反铁磁态。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了对称性约化如何驱动临界点分叉并产生deconfined量子临界线及浮游相，建立的相图为理解Landau不相容相变和连续变化的临界指数提供了新的范例。

Abstract: We investigate the cluster Ising chain with an additional nearest-neighbor interaction using tensor-network methods and a weak-coupling field theory. Without the interaction, the Jordan-Wigner transformation decomposes the model into a triplet of identical Majorana chains related by an exact $O(3)$ flavor symmetry. Their common mass vanishes at the $SU(2)_2$ Wess-Zumino-Novikov-Witten critical point with central charge $c = 3/2$ separating the symmetry-protected topological cluster phase from a ferromagnet. The interaction reduces $O(3)$ to its cyclic subgroup $C_3$, splitting the triplet into a singlet and a doublet whose gaps close separately, producing a critical bifurcation into Ising ($c = 1/2$) and Gaussian ($c = 1$) critical lines. A second ferromagnetic phase opens between these critical lines for repulsive interactions and a disordered phase for attractive ones. The Gaussian line then separates two Landau-incompatible ferromagnets, realizing a deconfined quantum critical line with emergent $O(2)$ symmetry, along which our independently extracted exponents vary continuously yet satisfy the parameter-free relation $β= (2ν- 1)/4$ of the eight-vertex weak universality class. At stronger repulsion this line opens into an extended gapless floating phase with incommensurate algebraic correlations, entered through Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions. All of these phases and the transitions between them are captured by the weak-coupling theory. Beyond its regime of validity, our simulations reveal a translation-symmetry-breaking antiferromagnet reached through first-order transitions.

</details>


### [146] [Influence of the inter-orbital interaction and kinetic terms on superconductivity: a simple two-orbital Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24337)
*Vito Marino,Diego Florez-Ablan,Luca F. Tocchio,Massimo Capone,Federico Becca*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含轨道内与轨道间最近邻跳迁（t和t̃）以及密度-密度相互作用（U和U'）的最小双轨道Hubbard模型，利用基于Jastrow-Slater波函数的变分量子蒙特卡洛方法，在均匀非磁态限制下考察电子配对机制。研究发现U/t=10时，轨道间相互作用U'和轨道间跳迁t̃均能显著增强超导关联强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚焦多层/多轨道体系中的超导电子机制，旨在分离并理解轨道间相互作用（U'和t̃）对超导配对的促进作用，以超越单轨道模型的描述能力。

Method: 采用基于Jastrow-Slater变分波函数的量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）技术，将变分态限制为均匀非磁态并显式包含配对振幅，系统扫描填充数和模型参数以计算配对关联函数。

Result: 在U/t=10处，轨道间项U'和t̃的存在显著增强超导关联。当t̃=0时，有限U'有效屏蔽轨道内排斥U，导致超导穹顶发生位移，轨道间排斥相比单轨道基线大幅提升电子配对。有限t̃的引入进一步增强了超导关联，这一效应由电子结构中平带与宽带共存所驱动。

Conclusion: 轨道间相互作用和跳迁是提升双轨道Hubbard模型中电子配对的关键因素，轨道间排斥U'通过屏蔽效应增强超导性，而轨道间跳迁t̃则借助平带-宽带共存的电子结构特征为超导关联提供额外增益。

Abstract: We investigate a minimal two-orbital Hubbard model with intra- and inter-orbital nearest-neighbor hopping $t$ and $\tilde{t}$, as well as intra- and inter-orbital density-density interactions $U$ and $U'$ by means of variational ansätze based on Jastrow-Slater wave functions within quantum Monte Carlo techniques. To focus on the electronic mechanisms of superconductivity, we restrict the variational ansätze to uniform nonmagnetic states with an explicit pairing amplitude and compute the pairing correlations as a function of filling and model parameters. At $U/t=10$, superconducting correlations are highly enhanced by the presence of inter-orbital terms, $U'$ and $\tilde{t}$. For $\tilde{t}=0$, a finite value of $U'$ effectively screens the intra-orbital repulsion $U$, producing a shift in the superconducting dome. Consequently, inter-orbital repulsion yields a sizable increase in electron pairing compared to the single-orbital baseline. Furthermore, introducing a finite $\tilde{t}$ provides an additional boost to superconducting correlations, an effect driven by the simultaneous presence of flat and broad bands in the electronic structure.

</details>


### [147] [Altermagnetic Anomalous Hall Effect and Spin--Edge-Locked Chiral Modes in a Modified Kane--Mele--Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24408)
*Mohsen Hafez-Torbati,Alireza Qaiumzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通过关联效应驱动的交错磁反常霍尔效应（AHE）实现方案：在修正的Kane-Mele-Hubbard模型中，通过Hubbard相互作用和空穴掺杂诱导自旋翻转相变，打破残余反幺正对称性，从而产生源于两个自旋对称扇区等量加和贡献的交错磁AHE及自旋-边缘锁定手性边缘态，为相关过渡金属硫族化合物单层中实现该效应提供了微观路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交错磁反常霍尔效应通常需要显式的Ising型各向异性相互作用或特定的自旋序构型；本文旨在探索是否仅靠载流子掺杂与自旋旋转不变的Hubbard相互作用即可实现交错的磁AHE，从而为实际材料中该效应提供更普遍、更易实现的微观机制。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）对修正的Kane-Mele-Hubbard模型进行分析；系统研究半填充时增大Hubbard相互作用导致的金属顺磁相（具有反手性边缘态）到面内Néel型反铁磁绝缘相的转变；随后通过空穴掺杂考察自旋翻转跃迁至面外Néel反铁磁相，并分析该相变如何打破残余反幺正对称性以产生有限的反常霍尔电导率。

Result: 研究发现：半填充时Hubbard相互作用驱动系统进入面内Néel反铁磁绝缘相，其中残余反幺正对称性禁止AHE；空穴掺杂引发自旋翻转跃迁至面外Néel相，打破该对称性并在强关联区仍产生有限反常霍尔电导率；与铁磁体中常规自旋极化霍尔响应不同，该交错磁AHE来自两个对称相关自旋扇区的等量加和贡献，并伴随自旋-边缘锁定手性边缘态。

Conclusion: 载流子掺杂与自旋旋转不变的Hubbard相互作用足以实现交错磁反常霍尔效应，无需引入显式Ising型相互作用；这为在相关过渡金属硫族化合物单层中实现交错磁AHE提供了一条现实可行的微观途径。

Abstract: We establish a correlation-driven route to the altermagnetic anomalous Hall effect (AHE) and its associated \emph{spin--edge-locked} edge states in a modified Kane--Mele--Hubbard model. Using dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), we show that, at half-filling, increasing the Hubbard interaction drives the system from a metallic paramagnetic phase hosting antichiral edge states into an insulating in-plane Néel-type antiferromagnetic phase, in which a residual antiunitary symmetry forbids the AHE. Hole doping induces a spin-flop transition to an out-of-plane Néel-type antiferromagnetic phase, thereby breaking this symmetry and generating a finite anomalous Hall conductivity that persists into the strongly correlated regime. Distinct from a conventional spin-polarized Hall response in ferromagnets, the altermagnetic AHE receives equal and additive contributions from the two symmetry-related spin sectors and is accompanied by spin--edge-locked chiral states. Our results demonstrate that carrier doping and spin-rotationally invariant Hubbard interactions are sufficient to realize the altermagnetic AHE, without invoking an explicitly Ising-like interaction, and provide a realistic microscopic route toward its realization in correlated transition metal dichalcogenides monolayers.

</details>


### [148] [Anomalous magnetocaloric effects in the quasi-one-dimensional antiferromagnet BaCo$_2$V$_2$O$_8$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24463)
*Jiahao Yang,Chao Dong,Xinlong Shi,Zhuo Wang,Tiantian Li,Liusuo Wu,Junfeng Wang,Zhangzhen He,Liang Li,Yongkang Luo,Jianda Wu*

TL;DR: 本文研究了准一维伊辛类反铁磁体BaCo₂V₂O₈在横向场下的热力学性质，发现其倾斜螺旋链几何和各向异性Lande g张量产生了空间调制的塞曼耦合。角度分辨磁热效应测量揭示了在H∥[110]横向场伊辛临界场附近存在高温最小值，且该最小值在磁场旋转时保持并仅微弱移动。张量网络计算表明旋转诱导的交错横向场迅速降低伊辛临界场，磁Grüneisen比在高温最小值附近改变符号，与实验一致。结果表明主导磁热响应可在伊辛临界区域之外持续存在，为通过定制量子磁体中的各向异性塞曼耦合构型实现磁致冷提供途径。
