  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索偏离伊辛临界区域时磁热效应（MCE）是否仍能保持主导响应，从而为量子磁体中的磁致冷提供新的物理机制和调控维度。通过在具有倾斜螺旋链几何和各向异性Landé g张量的准一维量子磁体中研究横向场热力学，揭示旋转诱导的交错横向场对临界场和Grüneisen比的影响。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：(1)角度分辨磁热效应（MCE）测量，系统扫描磁场方向与幅值对温度响应的影响；(2)张量网络数值模拟（tensor-network calculations），计算旋转诱导的交错横向场下伊辛临界场的变化及磁Grüneisen比的行为。

Result: (1) 在H∥[110]方向伊辛临界场附近观测到高温最小值，该最小值在磁场旋转时持续存在且仅微弱移动；(2) 旋转诱导的交错横向场使伊辛临界场迅速降低；(3) 磁Grüneisen比在高温最小值附近发生符号改变；(4) 理论计算与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 主导的磁热响应可以脱离伊辛临界区域而持续存在，各向异性塞曼耦合构型的调控为量子磁体中的磁致冷提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the transverse-field thermodynamics of the quasi-one-dimensional Ising-like antiferromagnet BaCo$_2$V$_2$O$_8$, whose tilted screw-chain geometry and anisotropic Landé $g$ tensor generate spatially modulated Zeeman couplings. Angle-resolved magnetocaloric-effect (MCE) measurements reveal a high-field temperature minimum near the transverse-field Ising critical field for $H\parallel[110]$ that persists and shifts only weakly upon field rotation. Tensor-network calculations show that the rotation-induced staggered transverse field rapidly lowers the Ising critical field and that the magnetic Grüneisen ratio changes sign near the high-field temperature minimum, consistent with experiment. Our results establish that a dominant MCE response can persist away from the Ising critical region, suggesting a route to magnetic cooling by tailoring anisotropic Zeeman-coupling configurations in quantum magnets.

</details>


### [149] [Thermal DMRG for quasi one-dimensional magnetic molecules and chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24608)
*L. Horstmann,J. Schnack*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文使用TenPy套件评估密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）方法在具有复杂相互作用模式的准一维磁分子体系中计算热平衡磁学可观测量的精度，重点关注那些超出精确对角化或Krylov空间方法能力范围的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然DMRG/tensor network方法在一维量子自旋系统（最近邻交换、开放边界条件）中收敛性优异，但需要验证这些方法能否推广到具有更复杂相互作用模式的磁分子体系，以计算磁性可观测量的热平衡性质。

Method: 使用TenPy套件，对多个典型的准一维结构进行DMRG和tensor network计算，评估其在热平衡性质（磁学可观测量）计算中的实际精度。

Result: 研究确定了DMRG方法在准一维复杂相互作用磁分子体系中的实际可达到精度，特别针对那些无法用精确对角化或Krylov空间方法处理的大尺寸系统。

Conclusion: DMRG/tensor network方法有望扩展到具有复杂相互作用模式的准一维磁分子体系的热平衡性质计算，但实际精度取决于具体的相互作用模式和系统尺寸。

Abstract: Density matrix renormalization group methods or tensor network methods in general can not only be used to determine ground states but also to evaluate thermal equilibrium properties. Although convergence is superior for one-dimensional quantum spin systems with nearest neighbor exchange and open boundary conditions, the hope is that these methods can as well be employed to approximate magnetic observables of magnetic molecules with more complex interaction patterns. Here, we study the accuracy that can realistically be achieved for several archetypical quasi one-dimensional structures using the TenPy suite. In our study, we aim at systems that are too large for exact diagonalization or Krylov space methods.

</details>


### [150] [Accidental accuracy and vertex corrections in $GW$: Exact benchmarks for the extended Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24838)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过半填充扩展Hubbard模型的精确对角化作为数值精确参考，构建了相同希尔伯特空间上的模型空间GW理论，系统量化了GW近似在相关体系中的误差。研究发现顶点修正随相互作用强度改变特征：弱耦合区有效顶点Γ_eff<1（反映短程关联对RPA电荷涨落的抑制），Mott区Γ_eff单调增长至~3（反映打开Hubbard隙所需的局域顶点）；电子-空穴通道的顶点修正反而恶化带隙误差，表明Mott隙位于自能通道。静态COHSEX在V=0时存在单个偶然精确交叉点U*≈3.5t，有限V通过非局域Fock交换将其分裂为向弱耦合塌缩的双交叉窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW近似是材料准粒子预测的标准工具，但其在强相关体系中的有效性范围一直缺乏定量刻画，因为从头算顶点修正的计算成本过高。作者希望利用可数值精确求解的小型模型系统，构建精确参考并量化GW近似的系统性误差来源。

Method: 采用半填充扩展Hubbard模型（含局域U和近邻V）在有限环上的精确对角化作为数值精确基准；在同一希尔伯特空间中构造模型空间GW理论，通过对比两者提取有效顶点Γ_eff；分别考察弱耦合区和Mott区的顶点修正行为，并分析COHSEX静态近似在参数空间的精确性。

Result: （1）弱耦合下Γ_eff<1，反映精确短程关联抑制RPA电荷涨落；（2）Mott区Γ_eff单调增长至~3（N=6），对应打开Hubbard隙所需的局域顶点强度；（3）极化率通道的顶点修正会恶化带隙误差，表明Mott隙产生于自能通道；（4）V=0时COHSEX在U*≈3.5t处偶然精确；（5）有限V通过非局域Fock交换将单交叉点分裂为双交叉窗口并向弱耦合方向移动。

Conclusion: 该工作为GW在相关材料中的可靠性提供了定量诊断标准，揭示了GW误差的通道依赖性，并指出了COHSEX类静态近似的有效性边界。顶点修正对带隙的影响并非总是改善，其正确渠道（自能而非极化率）对改进GW方法具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: The $GW$ approximation is the standard tool for quasiparticle predictions in materials, yet its regime of validity in correlated systems remains poorly quantified, because \textit{ab initio} vertex corrections are computationally prohibitive. Using exact diagonalization of the half-filled extended Hubbard model on finite rings as a numerically exact reference, we construct the corresponding model-space $GW$ theory on the identical Hilbert space and quantify its error as a function of local ($U$) and non-local ($V$) interaction strength. We find that the vertex correction changes character across the phase diagram: in the weak-coupling regime the effective vertex $Γ_{\rm eff} < 1$, reflecting the suppression of RPA charge fluctuations by exact short-range correlations, whereas in the Mott regime $Γ_{\rm eff}$ grows monotonically (to $\sim 3$ for $N=6$, reflecting the local vertex required to open the Hubbard gap. Vertex corrections in the electron-hole (polarizability) channel are shown to \emph{worsen} the gap error, indicating that the Mott gap resides in the self-energy channel. For $V=0$, static $COHSEX$ is accidentally exact at a single crossover point $U^* \approx 3.5\,t$; finite $V$, through non-local Fock exchange, splits this point into a double-crossover window that collapses toward weak coupling. These results yield quantitative diagnostics for the reliability of $GW$ in correlated materials.

</details>
